### A18. "Today" range pill on parent "My Wellbeing" showed a mislabeled 7-day count — root-caused, fixed, and live-verified — ✅ DONE 2026-08-19
**File:** `frontend/app/parent/my-wellbeing.tsx` (`loadData`)

**Reported by Jono, real device:** family/parent screen showed "3 check-ins today" for the family member Madalena when only 1 real check-in actually happened. Turned out not to be the family dashboard (`parent/dashboard.tsx`) at all — traced to the per-member "My Wellbeing" screen (`my-wellbeing.tsx`), reached via each adult family member's "Wellbeing" button.

**Scope check done first:** verified `parent/dashboard.tsx`'s own multi-source check-in fetch (`fetchMemberData`, covers `relationship==='child'` family members + school-linked children) separately — its 3 backend calls (`GET /zone-logs/student/{id}`, `GET /family/zone-logs/{id}`, `GET /parent/linked-child/{id}/all-checkins`) all correctly declare and honor a `days: int` query param matching what the frontend sends. **That path is unaffected** — this bug is isolated to the one `my-wellbeing.tsx` screen. (Also ruled out: Madalena is `relationship: 'partner'`, not `'child'`, so she was never in `fetchMemberData`'s scope in the first place — this initially misdirected the investigation toward the wrong file before the real screen was found.)

**Root cause: a query-parameter contract mismatch, not a fan-out or duplicate-data bug.** `loadData()` sent `?start=<ISO>&end=<ISO>` to `GET /family/members/{member_id}/checkins`, computed from whichever range pill (Today/7/14/30 days) was selected. The backend endpoint (`server.py:9690-9691`) only ever declared `days: int = 7` — it never read `start`/`end` at all, and FastAPI silently drops query params that don't match a declared parameter. Every range pill therefore always returned the same hardcoded last-7-days window, mislabeled with whatever pill happened to be selected. Confirmed live before fixing: hit the endpoint with the app's exact "Today" request (a real 24-hour window) and it still returned 3 rows — 1 from today plus 2 from exactly 7 days earlier, which is precisely what Jono saw.

**Fix:** `loadData()` already computed `const days = parseInt(range)` locally one line above the fetch — it just wasn't being sent. Changed the request to `?days=${days}`, matching the `days: int` convention every other check-in endpoint in this codebase already uses. Confirmed via grep this is the *only* caller of this endpoint sending `start`/`end` — the endpoint's other two callers (`linked-child/[id].tsx`, `family-member-stats/[id].tsx`) already correctly sent `days=`, so this was an isolated outlier, not a wider pattern. No backend change needed — the endpoint already correctly implemented `days`, it just never received it.

**Verified:** diff reviewed before applying (4-line change, isolated to the one fetch call, no other logic touched); `tsc --noEmit` shows only the same pre-existing, unrelated `useWindowDimensions` error already present in this file all session. This is a frontend-only change — no backend deploy needed. Live-tested all 4 range pills against production with the real fixed request shape: `days=1` → 1 (correct, was 3), `days=7` → 3, `days=14` → 4, `days=30` → 4 — each now genuinely distinct and matching the real underlying data, where before every pill silently returned the same number. Committed and pushed (`b78d20d`).

# APP STORE / PLAY STORE READINESS
*(Audit only, 2026-08-19 — TestFlight targeted this weekend, App Store/Play Store after TestFlight feedback. Nothing fixed yet, per Jono's instruction — this is the findings + decision list.)*

### S1. Account deletion — ✅ DONE 2026-08-20, built and safety-tested; blocked on one manual migration
Originally found missing (no backend endpoint, no UI, thorough grep across `server.py`/app/portal — zero real hits), then **re-verified from scratch a second time** after Jono flagged a contradictory claim from earlier the same night that it "fully exists" — re-confirmed exhaustively (every one of the 18 real `DELETE`-verb routes checked individually, none touch `users`; broad name/text sweeps across backend, frontend, and portal — zero matches anywhere). The earlier "fully exists" claim was wrong; likely confused with the real-but-different "unlink" feature (severs a relationship, not an account).

**Why this blocks submission, not just "nice to have":** Apple's App Store Review Guideline 5.1.1(v) (enforced since June 2022) requires any app that supports account creation to also offer in-app account deletion — an email-to-support path does not satisfy it. Google Play has an equivalent Account Deletion requirement.

**Decisions (Jono):**
1. **Teacher:** personal data only (login/name/email/push_token). Classrooms/students reassigned to their linked school_admin immediately at request time. Blocked with a contact-support message if no school_admin is linked.
2. **School_admin:** blocked entirely for now — real support-assisted process only, no cascade logic.
3. **Soft-delete, 30-day grace period**, matching the Privacy Policy's own retention language. Immediate session revocation + login block on request; a scheduled purge job does the actual hard-delete after 30 days. Requires re-authentication (password or fresh Google re-auth) before the request is accepted.

**What was actually built:** `POST /account/delete-request` (role gate → re-auth check → **writes `deletion_requested_at` first**, before any other mutation — deliberate ordering so a missing-column failure, see below, can't leave a partial state where classrooms got reassigned or a subscription got cancelled but the account was never actually marked → teacher classroom/student reassignment → Stripe subscription cancellation, found and closed a real adjacent gap since nothing in this app previously cancelled a subscription on account closure at all → session revocation). `POST /admin/process-account-deletions` (superadmin-gated purge job, same "call this via a scheduled job" pattern as the existing `/creatures/notify-expiry` endpoint — needs an external cron/trigger, this app has no internal scheduler). Login blocks added to both `/auth/email-login` and `/auth/google` for accounts with a pending deletion. Frontend: a real "Danger Zone" section in `settings.tsx`, teacher/parent only (school_admin/superadmin see a contact-support message instead of a non-functional button), reusing the same Google Sign-In client IDs already used for login.

**Real infrastructure blocker found and worked around, not hidden:** this environment has REST API access only (`SUPABASE_URL`/`SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY`) — no direct Postgres connection, no SQL-exec RPC function (checked — none exist), so **no way to run schema changes (DDL) from code**. The feature needs one new column:
```sql
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN deletion_requested_at timestamptz;
```
**Jono needs to run this once in the Supabase SQL Editor** — small, purely additive, doesn't touch existing data. Until then, the endpoint fails safely (see ordering above) rather than partially.

**Verified live, deliberately limited to zero-side-effect paths given how destructive this feature is** — did not trigger a real deletion against any account without explicit go-ahead: school_admin correctly blocked (403, no write attempted — role check happens before any mutation), superadmin correctly blocked (403), no-auth correctly rejected (401), and a real parent account given a deliberately wrong password correctly rejected re-authentication (403) with **zero side effects confirmed** — the same account logged in normally immediately afterward. The actual success path (correct re-auth → real soft-delete) has not been tested end-to-end against any account — that's a deliberate choice, not an oversight, and needs either the migration confirmed + a designated test account, or Jono running it himself. `tsc --noEmit`: zero new errors (same 52 pre-existing baseline as all session). `python3 -m py_compile`: clean. Committed and pushed (`2a534ee`).

**Known gaps, not covered by this pass:** no way to cancel a pending deletion within the 30 days except contacting support directly (no self-service "undo," no email confirmation link — would need real email-sending infrastructure this app doesn't have); teacher-owned artifacts beyond the literal "personal data" scope (resources they uploaded, `admin_teacher_strategies` they created, their own `teacher_checkins` self-check-in history) are untouched by either the request or purge flow — deliberately not decided on, flagging rather than guessing.

### S2. Privacy Policy / Terms of Service — ✅ both real and substantive; accuracy fixes drafted, not yet applied
**Correction to the initial audit pass:** Jono's message described "Terms of Service missing entirely." Re-verified live directly in response — `classofhappiness.com/terms.html` returns `200`, titled "Terms of Service — Class of Happiness," and is a genuinely complete 13-section document (who can use the service, accounts, subscriptions/Stripe, acceptable use, liability, safeguarding, governing law, contact). Not missing, not thin. Flagging this discrepancy rather than silently recording either version as fact — possibly Jono meant something else (e.g. a distinct App Store Connect EULA field, which is a separate, genuinely-optional Apple setting independent of having a website page) or simply hadn't seen the current live site. Both `privacy-policy.html` (~13,000 characters) and `terms.html` (~8,100 characters) are real, well-structured, non-boilerplate documents — fetched and read in full, not sampled.

**Real accuracy gaps found on close reading (not fabricated for effect — every one below is a specific line checked against actual code):**
1. **Google Sign-In is never disclosed anywhere in the Privacy Policy** — it's real and implemented (`frontend/app/auth/login.tsx:14-17`, real Google Cloud project `691097467706`), but neither the "What Data We Collect" nor "Data Sharing & Third Parties" sections mention it.
2. **Push notification recipients are described incompletely.** The policy says alerts go "to teachers only, where enabled." The actual code (`server.py:5629-5660`) deliberately and correctly separates *school*-context check-ins (→ teacher) from *home*-context check-ins (→ linked parents/family) — a real, well-built feature the policy doesn't mention at all. Not wrong, just incomplete.
3. **Retention/deletion claims aren't backed by automation** (same root issue as S1): *"Student check-in data is retained for up to 12 months, after which it is automatically deleted... Upon account deletion, personal data is permanently removed within 30 days."* No retention/cleanup job exists in `server.py` (grep-confirmed), and there's no "account deletion" mechanism to trigger the second claim at all.
4. **Broken in-app link:** `about.tsx:142` sends users to `classofhappiness.com/privacy-policy` (no `.html`) — confirmed live, that exact URL **404s**. Real page is `/privacy-policy.html`.

**Drafted, not yet uploaded — for Jono's review:** wrote targeted accuracy edits (not a from-scratch rewrite; both documents were already good) directly into local copies of the real live pages, addressing gaps 1-3 above:
- Added a "Signing In with Google" disclosure + Google as a listed third-party processor, with a link to Google's own privacy policy.
- Corrected the push-notification bullet to describe both the teacher (school check-ins) and parent/family (home check-ins) recipient paths.
- Softened retention/deletion wording from unconditional automated claims to accurate ones: *"We aim to retain student check-in data for no longer than 12 months"* and *"If you request deletion of your account or data... we will process this manually within 30 days of your request"* — true today regardless of whether S1 gets built, and upgradeable to an automated claim later if it does.
- `terms.html`'s own data/privacy summary section already just says "you have rights... to delete your data" without an automation claim — checked, no change needed there once the Privacy Policy fix lands, since it's already consistent.
- Verified the edited Privacy Policy file parses as valid HTML (`html.parser`, zero errors).
- Saved as `/Users/jono/Desktop/privacy-policy-UPLOAD-2026-08-19.html`, same manual-cPanel-upload pattern as prior website fixes. **Not yet uploaded — needs Jono's review of the actual wording first**, then his own upload.
- `about.tsx`'s broken link (gap 4) — ✅ DONE 2026-08-19. Changed `classofhappiness.com/privacy-policy` to the real working `/privacy-policy.html`. `tsc --noEmit` clean (zero errors, not even pre-existing ones — only touched a text string). Committed and pushed (`bd8458f`). Requires a new app build to reach a real device/TestFlight; not visually confirmed.

### S3. Google OAuth Testing vs. Production status — confirmed by Jono directly; not independently verifiable from this environment
Confirmed what's actually configured in code: a real Google Cloud project (`691097467706`), three real client IDs (iOS/Android/web) wired via `expo-auth-session` in `frontend/app/auth/login.tsx:14-17`. Whether the OAuth consent screen is in **Testing** or **Production** is a Google Cloud Console setting (APIs & Services → OAuth consent screen → Publishing status) — not exposed through the Supabase service key or any API reachable without `gcloud` authentication, which isn't set up in this environment (`gcloud` isn't even installed here), so this was never independently verified by inspection — **Jono has since confirmed directly that it's still in Testing mode.**

**Real consequence while it stays in Testing:** Google caps it at 100 explicitly-added test users and shows an "unverified app" warning to everyone else — would silently block Google Sign-In for real users at any real scale, including TestFlight testers who aren't on the test-user list. **Action needed from Jono:** submit for verification in the console (may require the Privacy Policy fix in S2 and a few other prerequisites Google's own flow will specify) before general users can use Google Sign-In. Worth doing before or alongside TestFlight, not after, since testers hitting the "unverified app" wall would be a bad first impression.

### S5. Screenshots / store assets — not yet created (per Jono)
Reported directly by Jono, not independently checked (no access to a design/asset pipeline from this environment). Needed for both App Store Connect and Google Play Console listings before either can go live — screenshots per required device size, an app icon meeting each store's spec, and any promotional graphics Play Console requires. Flagging as a real, concrete pre-submission task, not investigated further here.

### S6. COPPA / children's-app classification — needs Jono's real decision, not a guess
This is a genuine legal-classification question with real compliance consequences depending on the answer, and per Jono's explicit instruction it's flagged here for his decision rather than asserted either way.

**What's actually true about the app's structure, confirmed via code (relevant context, not a recommendation):** students never create independent accounts or provide their own contact details — profiles are created and managed entirely by a teacher or parent, confirmed in `server.py` and matching the Privacy Policy's own description. This kind of "child use is supervised, the account-holder is always an adult" structure is architecturally consistent with how many EdTech apps position themselves outside the strictest "primarily child-directed" classification — but that positioning is a real legal call with real consequences (COPPA operator obligations in the US if classified as child-directed or with actual knowledge of collecting under-13 data; Google Play's "Designed for Families" program has its own strict ad/data rules if declared; Apple's Kids Category has its own separate requirements) — not something to infer from code structure alone.

**Needs Jono's decision, ideally with real legal input given the compliance stakes:** does Class of Happiness get declared as a children's/family app (Google Play target-audience declaration, Apple Kids Category) or positioned as an education tool for adults (teachers/parents/schools) with supervised student use — the classification affects both stores' submission questionnaires directly, and picking the answer that doesn't match reality carries real regulatory risk either direction.

### S4. Data collection inventory — real, pulled from the live 31-table schema, not a guess
Compiled for Apple's "nutrition label" and Google's "Data Safety" form:

| Category | What's actually collected | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Contact Info | Email, name | `users`, `family_members` |
| Identifiers | `user_id`, Expo push token, Stripe customer ID, session token | `users`, `user_sessions` |
| Photos | Creature-drawing photo uploads (4 stages per submission), custom avatar images | `creature_submissions.stage1-4_url`, `avatar_custom` fields |
| User Content | Check-in comments, creature names/descriptions, alert/support messages, wellbeing-tracker notes | `feeling_logs`, `creature_submissions`, `student_alerts`, `school_wellbeing_tracker` |
| Emotional/wellbeing data | Zone check-ins (blue/green/yellow/red) + free-text comments — the app's core data, and genuinely sensitive-adjacent (a minor's emotional state) — likely needs its own line item, not lumped into generic "usage data," on both platforms' disclosure forms | `feeling_logs`, `family_zone_logs` |
| Usage Data | Timestamps, language preference, subscription/trial status | `users`, most tables |
| Financial | Stripe customer ID + subscription status only — confirmed no raw card data anywhere (Stripe handles that directly, matching the policy's own claim) | `users` |
| Confirmed NOT collected | No location/GPS, no contacts. `students.device_id` column exists in the schema but is verified never read or written anywhere in backend or frontend — dead/unused, so the policy's "no device identifiers from students" claim is actually accurate in practice | grep-confirmed across `server.py` and the full frontend |

**Needs Jono's decision:** use this table directly to fill in Apple's App Privacy questionnaire and Google's Data Safety form — recommend treating the emotional/wellbeing check-in data as its own disclosed category rather than folding it into generic usage data, given how central and sensitive it is to what this app actually does.

# FEATURES BUILT (outside the original review scope)

### F1. Kids' voice recording playback on colour/helper check-in buttons — ✅ DONE 2026-08-19, live-verified
**Files:** `backend/server.py` (`GET /voice-clips`), `frontend/src/utils/voiceClips.ts` (new), `frontend/src/components/VoiceToggleButton.tsx` (new), `frontend/app/student/zone.tsx`, `frontend/app/student/strategies.tsx`, `frontend/app/settings.tsx`

Real narrated audio (Matilda in English, Mateus in Portuguese) now plays automatically when a student taps a colour on the check-in zone screen or selects a helper strategy. English and Portuguese only — the other 4 app languages silently skip playback since no clips exist for them yet, by design (same "missing key → skip" path used for unfinished Portuguese clips).

**Storage decision:** no new DB table. `GET /voice-clips?language=X` lists the `voice-recordings` Supabase Storage bucket directly and returns `{clip_key: url}` for whatever's actually uploaded — replacing or adding a clip is the only step ever needed; nothing else can drift out of sync with it (the kind of dual-source-of-truth drift this review repeatedly found elsewhere, e.g. A7, A9).

**Bucket reorganization:** the bucket Jono had already created (`voice-recordings`, not `voice-clips` as first proposed — caught and corrected before building) held 50 real `.m4a` files flat at the root, human-named (`Blue_emotions.m4a`, `Ajudar_um_amigo.m4a`, etc.), not organized by language or matching the 28 canonical `clip_key` IDs (4 colours + 24 helpers, from `DEFAULT_HELPERS`). Listed the full inventory live, mapped every file to its canonical key by content (2 ambiguous matches — `Drink_water.m4a`→`blue_2`, `Continua_assim.m4a`→`green_1` — confirmed with Jono before moving anything), and moved all 35 real clips into `{language}/{clip_key}.m4a` via the Storage move API. Fixed two filename hazards in the process: a literal `&` in `Squeeze_&_release.m4a` and a trailing space in `Emocoes_Vermelhas .m4a` — both gone once renamed to plain `clip_key` names. Left 15 non-matching clips (greeting/praise/closing lines — `Well_done`, `See_you_tomorrow`, `Como_te_sentes_hoje`, etc.) untouched at the bucket root; Jono confirmed these are for a future phase (check-in start/completion screens), not this build.

**Coverage after reorganization:** English 27/28 (missing only the `green` colour clip). Portuguese 8/28 (4 colours + 4 of 6 green-zone helpers; all blue/yellow/red helper clips still pending from Mateus) — matches what Jono described going in.

**Playback + mute:** `voiceClips.ts` mirrors the existing (but previously unused/unwired) `sounds.ts` pattern. One persisted setting (`AsyncStorage`, device-local — not synced to the account, since check-in devices are often shared classroom tablets) with two entry points: a Settings toggle and a quick per-screen mute button on both check-in screens, rather than two states that could drift apart. Helper audio fires only when a card is *selected*, not deselected. Both of the app's two different strategy-ID schemes (`blue_1` from the real backend data, `b1` from the frontend's hardcoded emergency fallback) are normalized to the same clip key at lookup time, so playback doesn't silently break depending on which source a strategy card came from.

**Verified:** `python3 -m py_compile` and `tsc --noEmit` clean on every touched/new file (zero errors, not even pre-existing ones). All 35 reorganized Storage files confirmed publicly fetchable (200, correct `audio/x-m4a` content-type) via direct HTTP checks before and after deploy. Live-tested `GET /voice-clips` against production for both languages post-deploy: English returns exactly 27 keys, Portuguese exactly 8, an unsupported language (`es`) returns `{}` cleanly. **Not audio-tested in a running app** — no simulator/device available in this environment, so playback itself (does it actually sound right, timing, volume) hasn't been confirmed — only that the URLs, manifest, and code are correct. Committed and pushed (`c5ba4cc`).

**Follow-up round (2026-08-19) — 4 real-device UI bugs found and fixed:**
1. **Settings Voice switch invisible** — not a rendering bug: `settingLeft`/`settingText` had no width constraint, so the Voice row's long description text overflowed uncapped and pushed the `Switch` off the visible card edge entirely. Short values elsewhere (e.g. "English") never triggered it, which is why no other row looked broken before. Fixed by adding `flex: 1` to both — bounds the text to the row width, restores normal wrapping, applies to every row in the file at once.
2. **Description text overflowing card boundary** — same root cause and same fix as #1.
3. **Colour screen mute icon overlapping the greeting card** — it was `position: 'absolute'` floating over the top of the screen, directly on top of the greeting card's corner (both anchored to almost the same spot). Fixed by moving it into the greeting row as a normal flex child instead of an absolute overlay, so it can't overlap anything regardless of name length or screen size.
4. **Helper screens: icon needed to be inside the coloured zone heading, not top-right** — moved into the existing `zoneHeader` box (the bordered, colour-tinted "outline" showing the dot + zone name) as a flex sibling pushed to its right edge. One shared component driven by `zone`, so this covers all four colour pages in a single change.

All four verified via `tsc --noEmit` (zero new errors in either touched file, same 2 pre-existing unrelated errors as all session). Not visually re-confirmed on a device — no simulator available in this environment. Also flagged, not fixed: the same description-overflow symptom exists on the Set Password screen and in admin emails — separate files/StyleSheets, pre-existing, unrelated to tonight's feature — logged as a new P2 backlog item, not touched here per Jono's instruction.

**Coverage completed (2026-08-19, later same night):** 21 more files uploaded — 20 real Portuguese helper clips (filling every remaining blue/yellow/red slot plus green_5/green_6) and the one missing English colour clip (`Green_emotions.m4a`). Inventory-checked and mapped against the 28 canonical keys before touching anything, per the same process as the first batch — all 20 Portuguese mappings were unambiguous 1:1 translations (two, `Beber_agua`→`blue_2` and `Pedir_ajudar`→`red_5`, flagged as "plausible not certain" pending Jono's confirmation; `blue_2` confirmed correct — "Drink Water" is the current real name, "Warm Drink" in `DEFAULT_HELPERS` is stale text needing its own separate update, not yet done). Two more filename hazards fixed in the move: a literal space in `Contar ate 10.m4a` and a trailing space in `Contar_ao_contrario .m4a`. Reorganized into `{language}/{clip_key}.m4a` the same way as before. **Both English and Portuguese are now 28/28 — fully complete.** Live-verified immediately after the move (no deploy needed, pure Storage change): `GET /voice-clips?language=en` and `?language=pt` both return exactly 28 keys.

`red_5`/`Pedir_ajudar` — ✅ confirmed correct by Jono on a real device ("Pedir Ajuda"/"Ask for Help", matches). `blue_2`/`Beber_agua`/`Drink_water` also confirmed correct earlier. Both ambiguous matches from this batch are now resolved.

**"Warm Drink" → "Drink Water" — ✅ DONE 2026-08-19**, see the dedicated writeup below (this section's own scope was just the filename↔clip_key pairing; the actual displayed text across `DEFAULT_HELPERS` and every other real name-resolution list was fixed separately, commit `21b0f27`).

**Real problem found by real-device listening, 2026-08-19-20 — Portuguese content/clip_key mismatch, distinct from the filename-pairing work above — ✅ DONE, fully resolved:** Jono reported the actual *spoken content* on several Portuguese helper clips didn't match their clip_key/label — not a bad recording, a mispairing. By ear: Blue all 6 wrong, Green 1&2 correct but 3-6 wrong, Yellow 1 correct but 2-6 wrong, Red all 6 correct. Investigated whether this was introduced by this session's own reorg work — ruled out: the Storage move API only renames, cannot alter file bytes, and re-checking every PT translation used for the mapping confirmed the semantic pairing was linguistically correct. Checked original upload timestamps (preserved through the move) as a possible ordering signal — dead end, all 20 files were uploaded in a ~2-second bulk window, no usable sequence in that window. **Conclusion: the mismatch predated this session's reorg — the files were already misaligned with their actual spoken content before being uploaded/renamed.** No file-metadata signal could resolve which content actually belonged where, so this needed Jono to listen to each wrong slot and report what it actually said.

**Jono's by-ear data revealed the real pattern — each zone's slots 2-6 independently shuffled among themselves** (slot 1 always correct in every zone), consistent with a per-zone recording/export-order mix-up rather than one global mechanical bug:
- **Blue** — two cycles: `(blue_2 → blue_5 → blue_3 → blue_2)` and `(blue_4 ↔ blue_6)`.
- **Green** — two swaps: `(green_3 ↔ green_5)` and `(green_4 ↔ green_6)`. `green_1` flagged separately (see below), `green_2` genuinely correct.
- **Yellow** — one connected 5-cycle: `(yellow_2 → yellow_5 → yellow_6 → yellow_4 → yellow_3 → yellow_2)`.
- **Red** — confirmed 100% correct, zero changes.

**Applied:** 25 Storage move operations (each affected clip staged through a temporary filename first, then moved to its final correct slot — necessary because several of these are closed cycles, not simple pairwise swaps, so a direct move would have overwritten content before it was relocated). Verified zero leftover temp files afterward and all 28 canonical PT keys still present. Live-verified: `GET /voice-clips?language=pt` still returns exactly 28 keys post-move, URLs resolve to the newly-correct paths.

**`green_1` — flagged, not touched:** audio says "Continua Assim" (keep going *like that*), expected "Continua" (keep going). Not part of the shuffle — no other slot's content matches it better, and nothing else is missing a home. This is a phrasing difference, not a mispairing; a rename can't fix it since there's no file to swap it with. Left as-is; a re-record is Jono's call if he wants it tightened.

**Bonus finding, same investigation — `red_1`'s displayed text didn't match its (correct) audio:** audio says "Ficar Parado" (Stay Still); the Portuguese helper name shown in the app said "Parar" (Stop). Jono confirmed the audio is right and the text should change to match, not the reverse. Fixed in both real Portuguese sources: `HELPERS_PT` (`server.py`, the live `GET /helpers?lang=pt` data — also fixed its dead, shadowed duplicate definition for consistency, since it's harmless and avoids future confusion) and `STRATEGY_NAME_MAP_PT` (PDF/report name resolution). Confirmed via grep this doesn't touch Spanish's own separate `"Parar"` entries (different language, unrelated, correctly left alone) and that no frontend/portal file hardcodes Portuguese strategy names — fully contained to two backend dicts. Deployed and live-verified: `GET /helpers?feeling_colour=red&lang=pt` now returns `"name": "Ficar Parado"` for `r1`; `lang=es` still correctly returns `"Parar"`. Committed and pushed (`2dc1eb2`).

**The Portuguese content-quality issue across the entire 28-clip helper set is now fully closed.**

**Not built yet, explicitly out of scope for this pass:** the 15 future-phase greeting/praise clips (F1 follow-up, whenever those screens get their own audio treatment — inventoried 2026-08-19: `How_are_you_feeling_today`/`See_you_tomorrow`/`Thank_you_for_checking_in`/`Well_done`/`Great_job`/`Check_in_with_my_feelings`/`You_did_it` all have EN+PT pairs recorded; `I_did_it` has no Portuguese counterpart yet); backend translation-dict entries for the new `t('voice')`/`t('voice_narration')`/`t('voice_narration_desc')` keys (currently rely on the English fallback string, same as many other keys in this app — not a functional gap, just unpolished for non-English Settings screens).

### F2. Custom strategies silently skip voice playback — needs a "why is this quiet" indicator, not a fix
Custom strategies (teacher/parent-added, per-student) will never have a canonical voice clip — `playVoiceClip` correctly no-ops for them (same code path as unsupported languages/unfinished clips), so nothing crashes, but a kid tapping one and hearing nothing has no way to know that's expected rather than broken. **Decision (Jono):** don't try to make it sound like a mute/error state — add a small visual marker on custom-strategy cards themselves (e.g. a "Made for you" badge, or a distinct border/colour) that explains the silence as a positive, on-brand touch rather than looking like a bug. Plan to be shown before building — see below. Separately logged as a real future feature, not building now: letting students/families record their own voice for a custom strategy, likely needing teacher/parent approval before it plays given child-safety considerations.

*(Explicitly deferred by Jono — record only, no work now.)*

### P1. Multi-currency / country-adjusted pricing
Launch is **EUR-only**. AUD figures currently exist in `SUBSCRIPTION_PLANS` (`price_aud` fields, `server.py:44-79`) as leftover/parallel values — leave as-is for now, don't extend or fix them as part of L2/L3. Deferred work for later: real multi-currency support, purchasing-power-adjusted pricing by country, and a public-vs-private-school pricing distinction. For now, affordability cases are handled manually via NGO grants and ad hoc discounts, not through product-level pricing tiers.

### P2. UX/polish backlog — from real-device testing, 2026-08-20
Record only, no investigation or design done yet. All raised by Jono during real-device testing tonight, alongside A18/A19 above.

- **Creature submission placement** — ✅ DONE 2026-08-19. Removed the standalone "Submit Creature" shortcut from `parent/dashboard.tsx` and `teacher/dashboard.tsx`; added a persistent "Submit a Creature" button inside the shared `CreatureManagement` component instead (used by both `/parent/creature-code` and `/teacher/creature-code`), so one change covered both surfaces. `tsc --noEmit` clean on all 3 touched files (zero errors, not even pre-existing ones). Committed and pushed (`655ca44`). Not visually confirmed on a device.
- **Classroom code — copy-to-clipboard.** Add a copy icon next to the classroom join code so teachers/parents don't have to manually retype it.
- **Missing loading indicator, Family dashboard.** No emoji/spinner loading state shown while the family dashboard's data is fetching — screen likely appears blank or frozen during load.
- **School contact details not editable.** Neither superadmin nor school_admin currently has a way to edit a school's contact details (address/phone/email or similar) — needs a real UI path, not currently exposed anywhere in app or portal.
- **Analytics: school-specific stats + colour-over-time graph.** Current analytics are aggregate; want the ability to drill into a single school's stats specifically, plus a graph showing zone-colour trends over time (not just point-in-time counts).
- **Future idea: "team sharing" PDFs.** Rough concept, not scoped — some way to share PDF reports with a wider team/group rather than one-to-one. Needs real scoping before it's actionable.
- **Scheduled project: Expo SDK 54 → 57 upgrade, flagged 2026-09-05 - not started, deliberately.** Expo Go's installed client has moved past SDK 54 (current project version, `expo@54.0.33`) to require SDK 55+, blocking Jono from loading the app at all - unblocked for now by installing the SDK 54 Expo Go client directly from expo.dev/go, buying time to do this properly rather than upgrading under pressure. Latest published SDK is 57. Explicit plan, confirmed with Jono: incremental, one major version at a time (54→55→56→57, each step `npx expo install expo@^N.0.0` + `npx expo install --fix`, tested and committed before moving to the next), on its own dedicated branch, not mixed into any other work. **Sequencing: do this AFTER both the Marisa design-review batch (`design/marisa-batch`) merges and the kiosk QR-pairing feature (currently stranded on unpushed local `main`, ships via its own PR - see A82) lands** - both are real in-flight work that would only get harder to rebase across 3 SDK majors the longer they wait. **Special attention needed: `expo-av`.** Already flagged deprecated as of SDK 54 (removal warning seen directly in this session's own `expo export` output) and is what the entire voice-clip system depends on - `src/utils/voiceClips.ts`/`sounds.ts`, every spoken check-in prompt and button sound, across all 10 languages (the exact system fixed for an overlapping-playback bug in A81). If `expo-av` is dropped entirely in one of these SDK jumps, this is real migration work to `expo-audio`/`expo-video`, not just a version bump - budget for it as its own sub-task within the upgrade, not an afterthought once something silently stops playing.
- **Part 2 (teacher/parent dual-access) open question, flagged 2026-09-04 - not touched, deliberately.** `subscription/index.tsx:83` gates the parent school-coverage check (`getParentCoverage()`) on `user?.role === 'parent'` - found during the sweep for other role-based guards while fixing the teacher-can't-reach-their-own-parent-dashboard bug (A81+ below), and deliberately left as-is since it reads as legitimate parent-specific business logic, not the same access-control bug. Real open question for when Part 2 (the "Restrict to Parent view" toggle, teacher accounts seeing both dashboards) gets scoped: should school parent-coverage apply to a teacher account's parent side too, if they have linked children? Needs a real decision, not an assumption either way.
- **Settings-row text overflow, pre-existing, wider than tonight's fix.** Fixing `settings.tsx`'s Voice toggle (2026-08-19, see F1 follow-up work) surfaced that its `settingLeft`/`settingText` styles had no width constraint, letting long description text overflow the card and push right-side elements (like a `Switch`) off-screen entirely — fixed in that one file. Jono separately confirmed the same description-overflow symptom exists on the **Set Password screen** and in **admin emails**, in files with their own separate `StyleSheet` definitions, not touched by tonight's fix. Not investigated — needs its own look at whichever screens/emails are affected before fixing.
- **Settings shows no "which school am I in" confirmation, and "Status" label is misleading for school-covered teachers — found 2026-08-20 during account-deletion live testing.** Two related gaps in `settings.tsx`: (1) once a teacher successfully joins a school, the "Join Your School" card just disappears (`settings.tsx:645`, gated on `!school_name`) — nothing replaces it with a positive "You're enrolled in: {school_name}" confirmation, so there's no UI anywhere showing which school a teacher belongs to. (2) The "Status" row (`settings.tsx:365-369`) only reflects personal `subscription_status` (`trial`/`active`/else "Inactive") — it has no concept of school-plan coverage (`school_admin_id` presence, used server-side e.g. `server.py:2583`'s `is_school_covered`), so a teacher fully covered by their school's plan still sees "Inactive." Same gap exists in the global `hasActiveSubscription` flag (`AppContext.tsx:640-642`) — also purely personal-subscription-based, no school-coverage awareness, so any other UI gated on that flag could misfire for a school-covered teacher too. Confirmed via live DB check that the underlying data is correct (`jono+deletetest@gmail.com` has `school_admin_id`/`school_name` both correctly set to Sunshine International) — this is purely a display/gating-flag gap, not a data bug, and doesn't affect account-deletion's reassignment logic (reads `school_admin_id` straight from the DB). Explicitly deferred — Jono was mid account-deletion test, didn't want it fixed tonight.
- **Portal "Users at Your School" always shows nothing for school_admin** — ✅ DONE 2026-08-21. Found 2026-08-20 during account-deletion live testing (confirmed pre-existing, unrelated to that test). `renderSchoolUsers()` (`portal100.html:1506`) called `/admin/users`, hardcoded superadmin-only (`server.py:8874`) — every school_admin call silently 403'd. Fixed by adding a real school-scoped endpoint, `GET /school-admin/users` (`server.py`, right after `/school-admin/analytics`), filtered server-side to `school_admin_id == caller's own user_id`; portal now calls that instead, with the now-redundant client-side filter removed. `py_compile`/`node --check` clean, committed and pushed (`3fdcb93`). **Live-verified** against `schooladmindemo`: returns the 4 real linked users (Sarah Mitchell, James Okonkwo, Maria Santos, plus the `jono+deletetest@gmail.com` test account — still listed since it's only soft-deleted, 30-day grace period, by design). Portal side uploaded to cPanel by Jono (`portal100-UPLOAD-2026-08-21.html`).
- **Portal "Class Performance Comparison" shows stale demo classes and hides real reassigned ones** — ✅ DONE 2026-08-21. Found 2026-08-20 during account-deletion live testing (confirmed pre-existing, unrelated to that test). `/school-admin/analytics` built its classroom lookup from linked `teacher_ids` only, never the school_admin's own `user_id` — so a classroom reassigned to them (e.g. via account-deletion's teacher-reassignment logic) was invisible, while empty pre-seeded demo classrooms (`demo_teacher_001/002/003`) still rendered. Fixed: classroom/student lookup now also includes the school_admin's own `user_id` (`classroom_owner_ids = teacher_ids + [user_id]`) — `teacher_ids` itself deliberately left untouched everywhere else in the function (total_teachers/seats_used/teacher_checkin_rate must stay teacher-only). Also fixed the early-return that skipped straight to "no data" whenever `teacher_ids` was empty, even if the school_admin had their own classrooms — now only returns empty if there's truly nothing (`not teacher_ids and not classrooms`). Added a real `teacher_name` fallback (the school_admin's own name) for classrooms they own directly, instead of generic "Teacher". `py_compile` clean, committed and pushed (`3fdcb93`). **Live-verified** against `schooladmindemo`: `classroom_breakdown` now includes `{"classroom_name": "Geography Class", "teacher_name": "Demo School Admin", "student_count": 1, "checkin_count": 0, ...}` alongside the 3 demo classes — the exact reassigned classroom (Geography Class / student Tom) Jono wanted to confirm was visible. **Update 2026-08-21**: this backend fix was correct, but Jono still saw zero bars for every classroom after uploading — traced to a SEPARATE, pre-existing portal bug (see next entry), now also fixed.
- **Portal "Class Performance Comparison" bar chart always showed zero bars for every classroom, real or demo, regardless of actual check-in data — found 2026-08-21.** After the classroom-inclusion backend fix above (confirmed correct via direct API test — `zone_distribution` had real non-zero numbers for demo classes), Jono still saw flat/empty bars for every class in the actual portal. Root cause: `portal100.html:1854-1857`'s Chart.js dataset read `c.zone_counts?.green` (and blue/yellow/red) — but the backend field is named `zone_distribution`, not `zone_counts` (confirmed in `server.py`'s `classroom_breakdown` construction). Since `zone_counts` never existed on the response, every `?.x||0` silently fell back to `0` — this has been broken for every school, not just Sunshine, and not something introduced by tonight's or 2026-08-20's backend work; purely a pre-existing frontend field-name mismatch, not a caching issue (a hard refresh would never have fixed it). Fixed: renamed all 4 lookups from `zone_counts` to `zone_distribution`. `node --check` clean, diff confirmed isolated to just this rename. Also confirmed separately: this chart never displayed a numeric "N students" figure anywhere — only coloured zone-count bars — so there's no separate "student count missing" bug, that's just what the (until-now broken) bars were supposed to represent. Needs upload: `portal100-UPLOAD-2026-08-21b.html`.
- **`/school/bulk-invite` ignored superadmin-supplied target school_id — same bug class as `/school/generate-invite-code`** — ✅ DONE 2026-08-21. Found while investigating why zero parents show for Sunshine in the new "Users at Your School" list: system-wide, only 1 parent account has ever had `school_admin_id` set (linked to Kairos, not Sunshine) — traced to `/school/bulk-invite` (the portal's superadmin Schools panel "📧 Bulk Invite" button) always using the CALLER's own `user_id`/`school_name`/`subscription_expires_at`, ignoring any `school_id` the portal sent, identical to the bug fixed in `generate-invite-code` (`a4f1a28`, 2026-08-20). Fixed the same way: superadmin can now pass `school_admin_id` in the body to target a specific real school (404s cleanly on a bad id); default (no target, or school_admin's own self-service call) unchanged. Unlike `/school/join`, this endpoint has no role restriction on the target account (can link a parent or teacher), so it's the only real path to link a parent to a school right now — `/school/join` was deliberately locked to `role=="teacher"` on 2026-08-20 (see A19/parent-teacher-linking fix above). `py_compile` clean, committed and pushed (`63bef66`). **Live-verified**: school_admin self-service still links to their own school (unaffected); superadmin with an explicit `school_admin_id` correctly links to that school, not superadmin's own account; superadmin with no target still defaults to superadmin's own id (old behavior preserved); bogus target 404s. All 3 throwaway test accounts cleaned up after verification. Note: the portal's Bulk Invite UI itself still doesn't pass a working target (same deferred "Schools & Codes" UI work as `generate-invite-code` — `school_id` it sends refers to the near-empty `school_profiles` table, not a real school_admin id) — this fix is backend-only infrastructure, consistent with what was deferred on 2026-08-20.

### Analytics Parity Audit — 2026-08-21, full cross-surface audit (school_admin/superadmin/teacher/parent × app/portal, PDF exports, expand/collapse)
Full audit run via 5 parallel research passes plus direct follow-ups, triggered by Jono wanting to pick the "comprehensive analytics" thread back up. Full writeup published as an artifact (not reproduced in full here — this entry tracks status/action taken on each finding).

- **`/admin/stats` leaked platform-wide data to school_admin, labeled "Your school's data"** — ✅ DONE 2026-08-21, launch-blocking, fixed same night it was found. Live-confirmed before/after: zone_counts went from summing to 1000 (whole platform, 90d) to 840 (exactly matching the portal's already-correct `/school-admin/analytics`). Also fixed in the same pass: classroom-ownership gap (school_admin's own directly-owned classrooms, e.g. reassigned via account-deletion, were invisible to this endpoint too — same fix already applied to `/school-admin/analytics`), and the app's own Users tab (`UsersManager` in `admin/dashboard.tsx`) calling the superadmin-only `/admin/users` directly instead of the new scoped `/school-admin/users`. One block (`feeling_logs` rows with a null `student_id`) deliberately left unscoped — that table has no teacher/user identity column at all, so it structurally can't be scoped without a schema change; disclosed in a code comment. Committed `84d46f2`, deployed, **live re-verified fresh** twice: `/admin/stats?days=90` and `/school-admin/analytics?period=90` return identical numbers (840/25/4) on the same account/window.
- **`GET /analytics/student/{student_id}` had no auth check at all** (`server.py:3217`) — ✅ DONE 2026-08-21, launch-blocking, fixed same night. Now requires login and mirrors the exact ownership pattern already used correctly on the nearby student-PDF endpoint (owns directly, owns their classroom, is a genuinely linked parent via `parent_links`, or a `family_members` link; superadmin always allowed). Committed `0dd8df0`, deployed. **Live-verified, 4 scenarios**: no token → 401; `schooladmindemo` requesting Tom (owned via their own reassigned classroom) → 200; `schooladmindemo` requesting a demo_teacher_001 student they don't own → 403; superadmin requesting the same non-owned student → 200.
- **`GET /reports/pdf/family/{family_member_id}/month/{year}/{month}` had no ownership check** (`server.py:4008`) — ✅ DONE 2026-08-21, launch-blocking, fixed same night. Added the missing `.eq("user_id", user["user_id"])` to the lookup; kept the failure as a 404 ("Family member not found") for both "doesn't exist" and "isn't yours," so the response never confirms whether a given id exists. Committed `0dd8df0`, deployed. **Live-verified, 3 scenarios**: no token → 401; `jono@gmail.com` requesting their own family member (Madalena, April 2026, real data) → 200, real PDF returned (116KB, valid PDF 1.4); same account requesting a family member owned by a different user → 404.
- **`GET /reports/pdf/classroom-overview` has no role check, only ownership-of-URL-user_id** — confirmed real but low severity (no cross-account leak, just doesn't enforce its own "teacher's classrooms" docstring). Not fixed, not blocking — logged for a future pass.
- **Portal's school_admin "🚨 N students need support" banner was undismissable and unclickable** — ✅ DONE 2026-08-21. `saAlertBanner` (`portal100.html:1613`) had zero interactivity — no `onclick` anywhere, unlike the identically-shaped `saColorTip` dismiss button right above it. Investigated the real/stale question too: school_admin has no separate "Alerts" nav tab at all (`buildSchoolAdminPortal`'s tabs are Dashboard/Resources/Strategies/Users/Wellbeing Tracker/Our Team/Services/Creatures/My Wellbeing) — "Recent Alerts" is just a card within the same Dashboard/overview page as the banner itself, so "click through" can only mean scroll-to, not a tab switch. Fixed: banner now `scrollIntoView`s to the `saAlerts` card on click. Separately confirmed **why the "24" number appears**, live via direct Supabase query: it's `students_needing_support` (distinct students with any 'red' log in the active period) from the already-correctly-scoped `/school-admin/analytics` — genuinely real data, not a bug, but it's 100% demo-seed artifact, not a live signal: all 24 of Sunshine's real (demo-seeded) students have at least one red-zone log somewhere in their history (170 total red logs, seeded 2026-07-30), so ANY period ≥30 days shows "24 of 24" — literally the entire school. The default 7-day "Week" view correctly shows 0 (nothing in the last 7 days); the banner only appears once you switch to Month/Term/Year. Not something to "fix" — just not a real live alert, a fully-saturated demo fixture. Needs upload: `portal100-UPLOAD-2026-08-21c.html`.
- **Superadmin portal's "Schools Breakdown" shows 0 check-ins this week for both schools** — confirmed genuinely correct, not a bug (checked same night). Sunshine's real seeded data is dated 2026-07-30 (outside any 7-day window); Kairos has two `school_admin` rows, neither with any linked teachers or students at all. Not the same bug class as Class Performance Comparison (that was a real field-name mismatch hiding real data — this is real data correctly reflecting a stale/empty dataset).
- **Superadmin portal's "Schools Around the World" map** — confirmed to be the same known, already-flagged `school_profiles`-table emptiness issue (`renderSAWorldMap(wwEl, saAllSchools)`, fed by `loadSASchools()`, comment explicitly says "real lat/lng from school profiles"), not a new bug. Deferred consolidation work, unchanged status.
- **No family-wide/"export all" PDF export** — confirmed real gap, only one family PDF endpoint exists (`/reports/pdf/family/{family_member_id}`, scoped to a single member). Post-launch, not building tonight.
- **Parent portal's "Family Emotions" donut has no time-window selector**, silently defaults to the backend's `days=7` fallback (both `/zone-logs/student/{id}` and `/family/zone-logs/{id}` called with no `days` param), and the scope label next to the heading only ever shows which child is selected, never the time window. Post-launch, not building tonight.
- **`jono+teacher@gmail.com` demo account was `role: "parent"`, not `teacher`, despite the login shortcut's name/PIN implying otherwise** — ✅ DONE 2026-08-21, flipped to `role: "teacher"` in the DB directly (was already linked to Kairos Montessori via `school_admin_id`/`school_name`, so no other change needed). Root-caused as a stale/mislabeled test account, not a portal bug — `buildPortal()`'s role dispatch (`portal100.html:410-423`) is a plain, unambiguous switch with no fallthrough; `buildTeacherPortal()` is real and full-featured. Zero real users were ever affected.
- Everything else from the audit (school_admin classroom-level view missing from app, superadmin's dead "Subscriptions & Revenue" section, orphaned `/admin/dashboard-summary`, teacher/parent app-vs-portal minor gaps, the PDF export matrix's remaining gaps — school-overview app-only, classroom-overview portal-only, superadmin has no PDF UI anywhere, teacher-wellbeing PDF picker app-only, expand/collapse never persisting) — confirmed real, all missing-feature/parity gaps rather than broken-or-insecure-data issues, correctly triaged as post-launch. Not reproduced in full here; see the published audit artifact for complete detail.

### School_admin wellbeing depth — 2026-08-21, three real gaps closed
Triggered by Jono explicitly reframing school_admin as "who they represent in real life" (principal/SEND coordinator/psychologist) and asking what that person actually needs to see across student/teacher/classroom/school levels. Full audit was reported inline (not reproduced here); status of the three real gaps found:

- **"Download School Report" was a print-dialog, not a real PDF** — ✅ DONE 2026-08-21. Root cause: the portal's only reachable button here (labeled "🖨️ Print" on the Dashboard's Emotion Check-ins card) called `window.print()` — never hit any backend endpoint. A second, separate half-built function (`exportOverviewPDF()`) existed but was never wired to any button — confirmed dead, removed entirely. Real fix: new `downloadSchoolOverviewPDF()` (portal), mirroring the already-working `downloadAuthPDF()` pattern used by classroom-overview/teacher-wellbeing, hitting `/reports/pdf/school-overview` (already existed, already worked — confirmed in the PDF audit). **Live-verified**: 200, real PDF, 113943 bytes.
- **Per-student click-through for school_admin — confirmed genuinely absent, not just unlinked** — ✅ DONE 2026-08-21. `openStudentDetail()` already existed (teacher/superadmin-only before this) but school_admin had no student roster to click from at all — `/school-admin/analytics` is deliberately aggregate-only, no names/IDs, by design. Built: new `GET /school-admin/students` (school-scoped roster with classroom names), a new portal "Students" tab reusing `openStudentDetail()`, and a shared `_is_authorized_for_student()` helper adding school_admin as a real authorized role. **Found and fixed in the same pass**: `/feeling-logs/{id}` and its two aliases (`/zone-logs/{id}`, `/zone-logs/student/{id}`) had **zero authentication at all** — same severity class as the two unauthenticated endpoints fixed earlier tonight — plus `/reports/available-months/{id}` had no auth either. All three now use the same shared helper. **Live-verified**: real roster returned (25 students incl. Tom via the reassigned Geography Class), analytics/feeling-logs/available-months all 200 for an own-school student, 404 (not a leak) for a bogus id.
- **Teacher-consent mechanism for individual wellbeing visibility — built from scratch, migration pending** — ✅ backend + app + portal DONE 2026-08-21, **not live until migration runs**. Previously there was no path at all, not even opt-in, for school_admin to see a specific teacher's wellbeing. Default-off, explicit opt-in, revocable any time — mirrors the existing parent↔teacher `home_sharing_enabled` pattern. New `users.teacher_wellbeing_shared_with_admin` column (migration below), `GET/PUT /teacher/wellbeing-sharing-status` + `/teacher/toggle-wellbeing-sharing`, `_school_admin_can_view_teacher_wellbeing()` gating both the existing teacher-wellbeing PDF and a new `GET /school-admin/teacher-wellbeing/{id}` JSON view. App: real toggle in Settings ("Share My Wellbeing", teacher-only, gated on already being linked to a school). Portal: shared teachers get a "💚 Shared" badge in "Users at Your School", clickable to an inline 30-day zone-count summary. **Live-verified the defensive fallback specifically** (migration not run yet): `GET` returns `{"shared":false}` cleanly, `PUT` returns a clear 400 "not available yet" instead of a 500 — nothing broken in the meantime.

  **Migration required** (not run by me — no DDL access from this environment):
  ```sql
  ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN teacher_wellbeing_shared_with_admin boolean DEFAULT false;
  ```

### Creature enrichment — 2026-08-21, backend + UI done, one piece migration-pending
Full gap analysis was reported inline before building (not reproduced here). All 5 requested items built:

- **Stages carousel, expiry badge, evolution count** — ✅ DONE, live-verified. `/creatures/global` now returns `stage2/3/4_url`, `times_fully_evolved` (per-creature count from `creature_unlocks` where `stages_unlocked==4`, grouped — same data/pattern already used in `/creatures/analytics`'s aggregate view, just per-creature now), and `featured_until` (only populated when actively featured, via a real join to `featured_creatures`). Portal: `renderApprovedCreaturesInto()` — a real image carousel (‹ › arrows, stage counter), evolution count, and a featured-expiry badge when applicable. Live-verified against the real "Waterman" creature: all 4 stage URLs present, `featured_until: 2026-08-31...` (correctly currently featured), `times_fully_evolved: 0` (real, no unlocks yet).
- **Superadmin view, same card, system-wide** — ✅ DONE. `/creatures/global` already had no school filter (confirmed, not a new change) — added an "✅ Approved Creatures" card to superadmin's Creatures tab, reusing the exact same `renderApprovedCreaturesInto()` function, not a separate/lesser view.
- **Classroom + which-student picker on submission** — ✅ backend + app DONE, **classroom capture not live until migration runs**. Confirmed live, via real data, that `creature_submissions.student_id` has always actually been the SUBMITTING ACCOUNT's own id (a real "approved" row's `student_id` resolves to a `users` row with `role: teacher`, not any `students` row at all) — so there was never a reliable way to know which child or classroom made a creature. Built: a real "Who made this? (optional)" picker on the submission screen (`submit-creature.tsx`) — teachers see their own students (`/students`), parents see their linked children (`/family/members`, only resolves a real classroom for school-linked ones). `/creatures/submit` accepts optional `real_student_id`/`classroom_id` with a defensive fallback (same insert-with-fallback pattern already proven working on the teacher-consent toggle above) so nothing breaks before the migration runs. Old submissions are NOT backfilled, by design.

  **Migration required** (not run by me):
  ```sql
  ALTER TABLE creature_submissions ADD COLUMN real_student_id text;
  ALTER TABLE creature_submissions ADD COLUMN classroom_id text;
  ```

### Creature superadmin scope-visibility bug — 2026-08-21 — ✅ DONE & LIVE-VERIFIED
Found live while testing the newly-built superadmin scope-editing dropdown: `/creatures/global`'s query unconditionally filtered `.eq("is_globally_available", True)`, and changing a creature's scope away from "global" (via the dropdown) sets `is_globally_available: False` — so the creature immediately vanished from the very card the dropdown lives on. Fixed: superadmin callers now see all approved creatures regardless of scope/availability; every other role keeps the original global-only filter. Confirmed live via `/creatures/global`: superadmin session saw a school-scoped creature that a non-superadmin session correctly could not.

### Student-facing creature eligibility + privacy fix — 2026-08-21 — ✅ trimmed scope DONE & LIVE-VERIFIED
Real gap: approved community creatures (`visibility_scope`: classroom/school/global) never actually surfaced anywhere a student could see them — `world-creatures.tsx` called a nonexistent `api.get` (no such export existed in `api.ts`) wrapped in a silent `catch`, so it always rendered the empty state regardless of how many creatures were approved. This directly explains the original "approved creatures don't show up anywhere students can see" report — it wasn't a missing feature, it was a genuinely broken screen. Separately, `/creatures/unlock` and `/creatures/my-unlocks` were real, working endpoints with **zero frontend call sites** (never wired to any UI), and `student/creatures.tsx` ("My Creatures" collection screen) was fully orphaned dead code — wrong endpoint URL, undefined `authToken`/`onBack` refs, zero call sites anywhere in the app.

Built (trimmed scope, per Jono's explicit call — scope-preference toggle and expiry-aware history snapshots deferred, see below):
- New `GET /creatures/eligible?student_id=` — real eligibility computation (classroom/school/global match against each creature's `visibility_scope`, resolving the student's classroom→owning teacher's `school_name`), replacing the broken `api.get('/creatures/global')` call in `world-creatures.tsx`.
- **Privacy fix** (explicit ClassDojo precedent, non-negotiable per Jono): `/creatures/eligible` never includes peer creator identity (`student_name`/`school_name`/`country`) — the admin-only `/creatures/global` (school_admin/superadmin moderation tooling) keeps identity, since that's a separate, appropriately-gated audience.
- `unlock_creature_stage` now optionally enforces eligibility server-side when the caller sends `real_student_id` in the body — a student can no longer unlock a creature outside their own eligibility (403), while old callers with no `real_student_id` are unaffected.
- Rewired `world-creatures.tsx` (real progress dots + unlock button per card, "My Collection" link) and rewrote `student/creatures.tsx` from scratch against the real `/creatures/my-unlocks` shape, now reachable from World Creatures.
- **Live-verified end-to-end** against real production data (Kairos Montessori's "Waterman" [global] / "Waterman 2" [school-scoped]): a Kairos student's `/creatures/eligible` correctly returned both; a different-school student correctly returned only the global one; the different-school student's unlock attempt on the school-scoped creature correctly 403'd ("This creature isn't available to this student"); the Kairos student's unlock attempt correctly proceeded (200, check-in-threshold message). No creator identity present in any `/creatures/eligible` response.

**Deferred to next session, explicitly logged per Jono's call** (not built tonight):
- Student scope-preference toggle (family/school/global/any) in "My Creatures" — confirmed genuinely absent from both `students` and `users` tables, needs a new column.
- Expiry-aware obtained-history — `creature_unlocks` only stores a final `completed_at`, no per-stage timestamps and no snapshot of the creature's state (name/scope/images) at the moment of obtaining, so a later scope change or removal would silently rewrite history. Needs new columns/a snapshot mechanism.
- Also flagged, not fixed: `TRANSLATED_HELPERS`' PT `red_6` entry (dead-code strategy dict, unreachable) shows "Conta até 20" instead of Self Hug — cosmetic, zero live impact.

**Two more real gaps found and fixed while honestly assessing "is this actually operational end-to-end" (Jono's explicit ask, not deferred — core to whether the feature works at all):**
- `unlock_creature_stage`'s check-in count queried `feeling_logs.student_id` by the caller's **account** id, but `feeling_logs.student_id` is always a real `students.id` (confirmed live, zero overlap between the two id spaces) — this endpoint had zero UI callers before tonight, so the bug was invisible; wiring it into a real button would have made it always say "do 5 more check-ins" forever, no matter how many a student actually did. ✅ Fixed & live-verified: uses `real_student_id` when the caller sends one (Mateus's real 3-of-5 recent check-ins correctly returned "2 more needed", was previously always "5 more" regardless of real activity).
- `creature_unlocks` was keyed only by the submitting **account's** `student_id` — meaning a teacher's whole classroom, or a multi-child parent's kids, shared ONE progress bucket per creature. Not an edge case — the common case for teacher accounts. ✅ Fixed: new `real_student_id`-keyed lookup/insert path (`_creature_unlocks_for()` helper), same defensive try/except-fallback pattern as tonight's other migration-dependent work, applied consistently across `/creatures/unlock`, `/creatures/eligible`, and a newly-added optional `student_id` param on `/creatures/my-unlocks` (also now strips peer creator identity, matching the `/creatures/eligible` privacy fix). **Live-verified the pre-migration fallback** (all three endpoints return clean 200s, no 500s) — full per-student persistence verification pending the migration below.

  **Migration required** (not run by me):
  ```sql
  ALTER TABLE creature_unlocks ADD COLUMN real_student_id text;
  ```
  **✅ Migration ran 2026-08-21.** Live-verifying per-student persistence surfaced a SECOND real bug: the table's pre-existing unique constraint `creature_unlocks_student_id_creature_id_key` is on `(student_id, creature_id)` — `student_id` is always the account id, so this constraint predates `real_student_id` and still only allows ONE row per account per creature, regardless of which real student it's for. Confirmed live: two students (Emma/Liam) under one teacher account, same creature — Emma's insert succeeded, Liam's insert 500'd (`23505 duplicate key value violates unique constraint`, full traceback captured via `railway logs`). Fixed the immediate danger — an unhandled 500 that would have silently corrupted the wrong student's row if retried naively — by returning an honest 409 instead of attempting a workaround. The **actual fix** needs one more migration (not run by me):
  ```sql
  ALTER TABLE creature_unlocks DROP CONSTRAINT creature_unlocks_student_id_creature_id_key;
  CREATE UNIQUE INDEX creature_unlocks_unique_progress ON creature_unlocks (COALESCE(real_student_id, student_id), creature_id);
  ```
  This keeps old (pre-migration) rows unique per account as before, while new real_student_id-keyed rows become unique per actual student — which is what makes two students on one account able to hold independent progress on the same creature at all.

  **✅ Migration ran 2026-08-21, live-verified end-to-end.** First re-test still 409'd; `SELECT conname, pg_get_constraintdef(oid) FROM pg_constraint WHERE conrelid='creature_unlocks'::regclass` confirmed the old constraint was genuinely gone, so the 409 wasn't the old constraint — re-tested immediately after and it passed clean (transient propagation, not a real ongoing issue). Full two-student isolation test against real production data (Emma/dstu001 + Liam/dstu002, same teacher account, same creature): both got independent rows, Emma progressed stage 1→2 while Liam correctly stayed at stage 1 throughout, confirmed via `/creatures/unlock`, `/creatures/eligible` (per-student `my_stages_unlocked`), and `/creatures/my-unlocks` (no peer identity in the response). Test rows and session cleaned up afterward — creature feature is genuinely done.

### Creature scope-preference toggle + expiry-aware history — 2026-08-21 — ✅ built, migration-pending
Both explicitly deferred earlier tonight ("skip for tonight, log as fast-follow"), then explicitly reversed and approved for tonight ("this is core to whether the feature is genuinely operational, not a refinement"). Built with the same defensive-fallback pattern as everything else migration-dependent tonight — nothing breaks before the migrations run (live-verified: `/creatures/eligible`, `GET .../creature-scope-pref` both 200 cleanly, `PUT .../creature-scope-pref` returns a clean 400 "not available yet" instead of a 500, `/creatures/my-unlocks` unaffected).

- **Scope-preference**: `students.creature_scope_pref` (new column), `GET`/`PUT /students/{id}/creature-scope-pref`. `/creatures/eligible` now returns `{creatures, scope_pref, has_classroom}` and narrows results to the stored preference — the preference can only narrow real eligibility, never widen it. Note: there's no separate "family" visibility_scope value in the data model (only classroom/school/global exist) — the picker reuses "classroom" as the closest real match, labelled "My Family" when the student has no classroom (a home-only family member) vs "My Classroom" when they do, rather than inventing a new scope value tonight.
- **Expiry-aware history**: new `creature_unlocks` columns (`creature_name_snapshot`, `emotion_colour_snapshot`, `stage_image_snapshot`, `was_featured`, `featured_until_snapshot`), captured once at the exact moment a student fully evolves a creature (stage 4). `student/creatures.tsx` prefers the snapshot over the live join once one exists, shows a "⭐ Limited Edition (expired)" badge when applicable. **Known residual gap, not fixed tonight**: `creature_unlocks.creature_id` has `ON DELETE CASCADE` to `creature_submissions` — actual deletion of a creature (not just a scope/feature-status change) still wipes its unlock history entirely, snapshot or not. No delete-creature UI exists anywhere yet, so practical risk is low, but this is a real gap in "history survives" if that ever gets built.

  **Migrations required** (not run by me):
  ```sql
  ALTER TABLE students ADD COLUMN creature_scope_pref text DEFAULT 'any';
  ALTER TABLE creature_unlocks ADD COLUMN creature_name_snapshot text;
  ALTER TABLE creature_unlocks ADD COLUMN emotion_colour_snapshot text;
  ALTER TABLE creature_unlocks ADD COLUMN stage_image_snapshot text;
  ALTER TABLE creature_unlocks ADD COLUMN was_featured boolean DEFAULT false;
  ALTER TABLE creature_unlocks ADD COLUMN featured_until_snapshot timestamptz;
  ```
  **✅ Both migrations ran 2026-08-21, live-verified end-to-end against real production data.**
  - Scope-preference (Mateus, real Kairos student): default `any` showed both Waterman [global] + Waterman 2 [school]; set to `global` → only Waterman; set to `school` → only Waterman 2; restored to `any` afterward.
  - Expiry snapshot (Liam/dstu002, 4 real unlock calls to reach stage 4 on the currently-featured Waterman): DB row confirmed `creature_name_snapshot: "Waterman"`, `emotion_colour_snapshot: "blue"`, `stage_image_snapshot` = real stage4 URL, `was_featured: true`, `featured_until_snapshot: 2026-08-31...` — all correct, and `/creatures/my-unlocks` returns them with no peer identity leaked. Test rows, sessions, and Mateus's preference all cleaned up/restored afterward.

### Creature unlock check-in colour-filtering bug — 2026-08-21 — ✅ DONE & LIVE-VERIFIED
Found via a direct investigation question, not a routine sweep: Jono asked what happens once a student fully evolves one creature, which surfaced that `unlock_creature_stage`'s check-in count had **no colour filter at all** — it counted every recent check-in regardless of colour toward whichever creature was being unlocked, so a blue check-in could progress a red creature. Confirmed the default per-colour creature system already does this correctly (`add_points`, `server.py` ~line 3158, routes each check-in's own `feeling_colour` to the matching creature via `FEELING_COLOUR_MAP`) and mirrored the same real pattern: `feeling_logs` is now filtered by `feeling_colour == creature.emotion_colour` before counting. Also closed a latent related gap: the creature record is now fetched unconditionally (previously only inside the `real_student_id` branch), so an invalid `submission_id` now correctly 404s regardless of caller.

**Live-verified** against a real student (Emma/dstu001, real 30-day check-in mix: blue=5, red=10, yellow=10, green=10 — 35 total): unlocking the real global "Waterman" (blue) creature correctly capped at stage 1 (needs 5 more blue-only check-ins for stage 2, despite 35 total check-ins available), while a temporary test red creature independently progressed to stage 2 using only its own 10 red check-ins — neither leaked into the other. Pre-fix, the same 35 total check-ins would have wrongly qualified *both* creatures for stage 4 regardless of colour. Test creature, unlock rows, and session all deleted afterward — production restored to its exact prior state.

### A20 — iPad tablet polish — 2026-08-21 — audited, deliberately NOT built tonight
Jono asked whether the app supports iPad, both at the config level and the real UI level. Findings, verified against real source:

- **Config-level iPad support already exists**: `app.json` has `"supportsTablet": true` (ios) — the app is already installable/runnable on iPad today, this was never actually iPhone-only at the build level.
- **Real UI risk on iPad: Medium** — "stretched phone app," not broken/crashing. Nothing structurally fails, but nothing was designed for the larger screen either:
  - **`Dimensions.get('window')` read once at module load, not reactive** — used in 15 files (`student/zone.tsx:12`, `parent/dashboard.tsx:80`, `teacher/dashboard.tsx:17`, `teacher/student-detail.tsx:34`, `parent/widget.tsx:13`, `kiosk/index.tsx:12`, `src/utils/globalStyles.ts:7`, `src/components/CelebrationOverlay.tsx:7`, others) to derive single-column, full-bleed element sizes (e.g. `teacher/dashboard.tsx:652` sizes a chart as `width - 80`). On iPad this doesn't break, it just balloons proportionally with no redesign to use the extra space. Only 3 of 43 screens use the reactive `useWindowDimensions` hook instead (`parent/my-wellbeing.tsx`, `parent/family-member-stats/[id].tsx`, `teacher/checkin.tsx`).
  - **Zero tablet-aware code anywhere** — confirmed via grep, no `Platform.isPad`/`isTablet` hits, no screen branches its layout for a bigger display.
  - **Modals have no `maxWidth` cap** — bottom-sheet style throughout (e.g. `teacher/student-detail.tsx:1427-1432`'s `modalContent`), stretches edge-to-edge (~1024pt) on iPad instead of centering at a sensible width.
  - **Creature grids fixed at `numColumns={2}`, no conditional** — `student/world-creatures.tsx:163`, `student/creatures.tsx:150` (both from tonight's creature-feature work) — on iPad this means 2 oversized cards with wasted whitespace where 4-5 columns would suit the screen.
  - **Safe-area handling is solid** (`useSafeAreaInsets`/`SafeAreaView` in 41 of 43 screens) — not an iPad-specific gap, ruled out as a concern.
  - **`"orientation": "portrait"` is set globally** in `app.json` — locks both iPhone and iPad to portrait-only. Worth an explicit product decision on whether iPad should support landscape, separate from the layout-polish work above.

**Explicitly deferred, not built tonight** — genuinely a separate session per Jono's call. For tonight's App Store submission: iPad screenshots left blank, shipping iPhone-only visuals; iPad users can still install and use the app (it isn't blocked), it just won't look tablet-native until this is scoped and built.

### A21 — default + community creature systems unified, one-active-per-colour rule — 2026-08-21 — ✅ DONE & LIVE-VERIFIED
Real bug found by live testing (Jono's daughter): fully evolving the default "Aqua Buddy" then tapping "Start" on a community creature appeared to work, but check-ins still fed Aqua Buddy, and going back into World Creatures showed no trace the creature had ever been started. Root cause, confirmed by reading `add_points` in full: `target_creature = FEELING_COLOUR_MAP.get(feeling_colour)` was hardcoded to the 4 default creatures — there was no code path anywhere for a community creature to ever become what check-ins feed into.

**The rule this settles** (explicitly reversing the earlier "can a student progress multiple creatures at once" finding from earlier tonight — the real answer is no): one active creature per colour; a student may only choose a new one once their current active creature for that colour is fully evolved; progress on the non-active one is blocked server-side, not just hidden in the UI.

Built:
- New `students.active_creatures` (JSONB, one slot per colour — holds either a default creature id or a community creature id).
- `add_points` now branches per colour: default ids keep the original points-based path unchanged; community ids route through a new shared `_progress_community_creature()` helper (extracted from `unlock_creature_stage`, removing ~80 lines of duplicated logic) using the real check-in-count mechanic.
- New `POST /creatures/start/{id}` — a genuine selection action (not progress), gated on eligibility + the current active creature for that colour being fully evolved first.
- `unlock_creature_stage` now also enforces "must be your active creature" server-side — closes a bypass where the endpoint could be called directly to progress a non-active creature.
- New `GET /students/{id}/my-creatures` — the real permanent "My Creatures" collection (Pokedex-style): every creature ever started/evolved, default or community, grouped by colour, real total-collected count. A creature leaving "active" status is never removed — progress rows are never deleted, so its final state (or the expiry-snapshot from earlier tonight) just stays as permanent history.
- New `GET /students/{id}/active-creatures` for frontend gating.
- Frontend: new `CommunityCreatureDisplay` (photo-based) alongside the existing emoji/animation-based `CreatureDisplay`; `rewards.tsx` branches between them. `world-creatures.tsx`'s interaction model changed from "tap to manually progress" to "tap to set as active" (progress now automatic via check-ins, matching how defaults always worked). `creatures.tsx` rewritten as the one real "My Creatures" screen — 4 collapsible colour sections (collapsed by default, matching the app's existing pattern), real total-collected count, a direct "🔍 Find a New Creature" shortcut (per Jono's follow-up: browsing/starting shouldn't require going through check-in first). Consolidates what used to be **three** separate, inconsistent "My Creatures" surfaces (a defaults-only modal on the reward screen, this screen's earlier community-only version, and nothing unified) into one.
- Two smaller real bugs caught and fixed in the same pass: the Bronze Shield badge could visually overlap the bouncing creature (creatureSection's greedy `flex: 1` bounded to a fixed `minHeight`), and the Continue button's background colour silently broke (`undefined`) for community creatures, which have no `color` field — now falls back to the real `EMOTION_COLOURS` palette.

**Live-verified end-to-end against real production data** (Mateus, a real Kairos student who already had Aqua Buddy [blue] and Blaze Heart [red] fully evolved from real usage) — full 7-step verification, all correct: starting Waterman persisted and was immediately readable back; My Creatures showed Aqua Buddy (frozen, `is_active: false`) alongside the new Waterman (`is_active: true`) with nothing disappearing; a real check-in correctly routed to Waterman while Aqua Buddy's points stayed frozen; attempting to start a second blue creature while Waterman was incomplete was correctly rejected (400); attempting to directly progress that second creature via `/creatures/unlock` was correctly rejected too (403, confirms the bypass-closing gate); `/creatures/unlock` still worked normally on the genuinely-active creature. All test state reverted to Mateus's original values afterward.

  **Migration required** (ran 2026-08-21, no DDL access from this environment):
  ```sql
  ALTER TABLE students ADD COLUMN active_creatures jsonb
    DEFAULT '{"blue":"aqua_buddy","green":"leaf_friend","yellow":"spark_pal","red":"blaze_heart"}'::jsonb;
  ```
