## A46 — iPad/tablet Phase 1: reactive responsive utility, Dimensions.get migration, modal width caps, 2026-08-26

Scoped honestly first (21/44 screens with a real tablet issue, three categories), Jono confirmed portrait-only and authorized Phase 1 only (foundation + modal caps), explicitly held Phase 2 (grid reflow) for a separate go-ahead.

**Shared utility, `src/utils/globalStyles.ts` — rewired, not new.** Found this file already existed with almost the exact right shape (`isIPad`, `containerStyle`, `fontSize`, `spacing`, threshold 768) — but was dead code, imported nowhere in the app. Rewrote it in place as `useResponsive()`, a real `useWindowDimensions()`-based hook (reactive to iPad Split View/Slide Over resize and rotation, unlike the old `Dimensions.get('window')` read-once version), still unused by anything yet — it exists now as the foundation Phase 2 will build on. Exports `isTablet` (768pt threshold, platform-agnostic — deliberately dropped the old iOS-only gate since a large Android tablet deserves the same treatment), `fontSize()`/`spacing()` scalers, `containerStyle`, and `modalCapStyle`/`MODAL_MAX_WIDTH` (480) for the modal work below.

**11 `Dimensions.get('window')` call sites migrated.** Real mix, not uniform:
- **2 were dead code** (`student/zone.tsx`'s `width`, `parent/dashboard.tsx`'s `screenWidth` — declared, never read anywhere in either file) — removed rather than "migrated," since there was nothing to fix.
- **4 were genuine chart-width props** (`my-wellbeing.tsx`, `teacher/dashboard.tsx`, `parent/linked-child/[id].tsx`, `teacher/student-detail.tsx` — all feed a `BarChart`'s `width` prop) — moved to `useWindowDimensions()` called inside the component; these charts will now actually resize on Split View/rotation instead of freezing at whatever size the app launched at.
- **3 were module-level pixel grid constants** (`kiosk/index.tsx`'s `CARD_W`, `parent/widget.tsx`'s `CARD_W`, `teacher/widget.tsx`'s `CARD_SIZE` — all feeding a fixed 3- or 4-column `StyleSheet.create()` card width). These are architecturally close to the Phase-2 grid work but were in the Phase-1 list, so handled narrowly: moved the same divisor (3 or 4 columns — unchanged, that decision stays Phase 2's) inside the component via the hook and applied as an inline style override, so the pixel width is now reactive without touching column count.
- **1 was already effectively tablet-safe** (`CelebrationOverlay.tsx`'s card already had `maxWidth: 350`) — migrated anyway for reactivity consistency, moved the `width * 0.85` calc to an inline style since it can't live in a static `StyleSheet.create()` block driven by a hook.
- **1 became the rewritten shared utility** (`globalStyles.ts` itself).

**10 modals capped.** 9 needed real changes (added `width:'100%', maxWidth:480` to the bottom-sheet/centered card style, plus `alignItems:'center'` on the modal overlay — needed together, since capping width without centering the overlay would've just left it stuck flush-left on a tablet, and centering without `width:'100%'` would've broken the existing full-bleed phone layout by shrink-wrapping to content). One (`parent/dashboard.tsx`) was already correctly capped at `maxWidth: 400` with a centered overlay from earlier work — left untouched. Spot-checked the two files with 3 shared modal instances each (`teacher/resources.tsx`, `teacher/student-detail.tsx`) to confirm none is an image/gallery viewer that shouldn't be width-constrained — all are form/list content, correctly in scope.

**Live-verified**: `npx tsc --noEmit` — same 23 pre-existing errors as the confirmed baseline (diffed error-by-error against a clean `git stash` baseline run; every difference was just a line-number shift from removed dead code, zero new or fixed errors). `npx expo export --platform ios` — clean bundle, no Metro/bundling errors. Confirmed via source read that every touched modal's JSX usage site actually receives the new style (not just the `StyleSheet.create()` definition).

**What still needs Jono's own device/simulator pass** (no simulator or browser in this environment, stated up front): whether the modals actually *look* right on a real iPad — the 480pt cap and centering is mechanically correct but unverified visually; whether the reactive chart widths resize smoothly during an actual Split View drag rather than just computing the right number; the 3/4-column kiosk and widget grids at their current column count on a wider screen (Phase 2 will address whether the column count itself should change, but even unchanged, the reactive pixel width should be checked on a real device).

**Not started, per Jono's explicit hold**: Phase 2 (grid reflow for `student/creatures.tsx`, `teacher/checkin.tsx`, `parent/checkin.tsx`, `student/submit-creature.tsx`, `student/select.tsx`, `CreatureCollection.tsx`) and Phase 3 (polish).

## A47 — Items 8 and 10, 2026-08-26

**Item 8 — Registered Schools vs Schools Breakdown, investigated, not merged.** Direct re-inspection (not relayed) confirmed the earlier finding still holds: Registered Schools (`schoolManagementHTML()`, CRUD over `school_profiles`) and Schools Breakdown (dashboard card, live-computed from `feeling_logs` via `/admin/stats`) are genuinely different — different data source, different purpose, neither a subset of the other. Not merged. Two cheap polish items built instead: (1) renamed "Schools Breakdown" → "School Check-in Activity" in the portal to reduce the confusing name overlap; (2) real cross-linking — clicking a school row in the Breakdown chart now calls a new `saJumpToSchool(name)`, switches to the Schools tab, scrolls to and briefly highlights that school's actual Registered Schools card (matched by name against the already-loaded `saAllSchools`, since the breakdown payload carries no school id). Portal-only — confirmed the portal has zero i18n infrastructure anywhere (no `t()`, no translations object; the one `coh-portal-lang` reference is unrelated, just a PDF-report language param) and Jono confirmed it should stay English-only, matching its existing pattern as an internal superadmin tool. Syntax-checked (`node --check` on the extracted inline scripts).

**Item 10 — World Creature Gallery ported (real); Subscriptions & Revenue fixed at the root, not just ported.**
- **World Creature Gallery**: trivial, genuine port. New `SectionCard` in the app's existing Creatures tab (`admin/dashboard.tsx`), reusing the `approved` array `CreatureModeration` already fetches from `/creatures/global` for the Approved Creatures list — no second API call. Groups by `country`, same 5-creature minimum threshold as the portal. Live-verified against real production data: 6 approved global creatures exist today, all `country: "PT"`, clearing the threshold — confirms the gallery will show real content, not just an empty state.
- **Subscriptions & Revenue — real bug found, not just a missing aggregation.** Both the portal's card and the app's own pre-existing (but identically broken) `SectionCard` in `SuperAdminDashboard` have read `active_parents`/`annual_parents`/`active_teachers`/`annual_teachers`/`active_schools` from `/admin/stats` since the day each was built — none of the five fields were ever computed or returned. Both surfaces have shown nothing but dashes/undefined in production the entire time. Built real, expiry-aware aggregation in `/admin/stats` (superadmin/admin only, gated off for `school_admin` to avoid leaking platform-wide revenue data to a single school): `active_parents`/`active_teachers` count `users` with `subscription_status == "active"` and a non-expired `subscription_expires_at`; `active_schools` counts `school_profiles` with `status == "active"`.
- **`annual_parents`/`annual_teachers` deliberately dropped, not faked.** Confirmed live before building: `/subscription/checkout` explicitly rejects any plan other than `teacher_monthly`/`parent_monthly` — there is no self-serve annual plan for parents/teachers at all. Worse, `_get_plan_from_price()` collapses any price ID to `"parent"`/`"teacher"` regardless of interval, and no `subscription_interval` column exists anywhere — so even a manually-created Stripe subscription outside the app's checkout couldn't be distinguished as annual. Building these two fields would mean inventing data that doesn't exist. Jono's explicit decision: build the real active counts, drop the annual rows from both portal and app rather than show fake zeros or force a premature migration for a billing tier that isn't purchasable yet.
- **Stale annual pricing removed from every surface that had it**, per Jono's explicit request, not just hidden: portal footer and the app's `pricingBox` both advertised "€39.99/yr"/"€59.99/yr" with no purchase path — changed to "(monthly only)". Audited for any other mention: `app/subscription/index.tsx` (the real parent/teacher-facing subscription screen) was already clean — a prior Aug 18 fix already removed placeholder annual figures there, confirmed via its own code comment. `AppContext.tsx` has one leftover dead `annual: "Annual"` fallback-string key with zero references anywhere in the app — harmless, not fixed, flagged as minor housekeeping. Backend has no pricing display, nothing to fix. **Marketing site (classofhappiness.com) checked live** (fetched directly, no dedicated `/pricing` page or sitemap exists) — its `openModal(...)` pricing copy only advertises real monthly figures (€4.99/month parent, €7.99/month teacher, contact-based School Plan); no annual pricing found there. Also caught and fixed a separate pre-existing bug on the same touched line: the app's `pricingBox` displayed `$` (USD) while every other real pricing surface in the app uses `€` (EUR) — corrected to match.
- New UI text (World Creature Gallery labels, the fixed Subscriptions & Revenue card labels, pricing lines) wired into real `t()` keys — 12 new keys added to all 6 language files (en/pt/es/fr/de/it), genuine translations not placeholders, strictly additive (verified via diff — the only line touched in each pre-existing file was the prior last key gaining a trailing comma).
- **Live-verified end-to-end against production**, not just read against source: deployed, then via a real superadmin session, created a temporary paying parent + paying teacher + active `school_profiles` row, confirmed `/admin/stats` counts moved by exactly +1/+1/+1, deleted all three test rows, confirmed counts returned exactly to baseline (4/4/0 real production numbers) with a second fresh session. Confirmed `annual_parents`/`annual_teachers` are genuinely absent from the response, not present-but-zero. `npx tsc --noEmit`: same 23 pre-existing errors, none new. Real `expo export`: clean bundle.
- **What still needs Jono's own visual pass**: the portal changes (cross-linking, rename, fixed Subscriptions card) only take effect once he uploads the file via cPanel, same as always; genuine on-device confirmation that the new World Creature Gallery pills and the now-populated Subscriptions & Revenue card actually render correctly isn't possible in this environment.

Committed and pushed (backend + app + translations: `93e94840`; portal edited locally, upload pending Jono).

## A48 — iPad Phase 2 (grid reflow) and Phase 3 (polish), 2026-08-26

Jono authorized proceeding without visual verification — his only iPad can't run the app at all (see the iOS 12.5.8 minimum-version conversation same session: real floor is 15.1, confirmed via the generated Xcode project). Explicit instruction: be conservative, ship on mechanical judgment + code review, flag anything genuinely uncertain.

**Foundation fix, found before Phase 2 could safely proceed**: Phase 1's `TABLET_BREAKPOINT` of 768 silently excluded the current iPad mini (6th gen, 2021) — it's 744pt wide in portrait, narrower than the classic 768 convention since Apple's 2021 redesign changed its aspect ratio. Lowered to 700 (safe margin under 744, well above any real phone width). Added `LARGE_TABLET_BREAKPOINT` (900) to separate "standard" iPads (mini/Air/Pro-11, 744-834pt) from the one meaningfully wider device (iPad Pro 12.9", 1024pt) for column-count decisions. Added to `globalStyles.ts`: `useDataGridColumns()` (2/3/4 columns for grids whose item count naturally grows with data), `useFixedGridColumns(itemCount)` (for a small FIXED count like exactly 4 zone buttons — stays laid out as on phone, becomes a single row on tablet rather than a 3-column reflow that would orphan a 4th item), and `gridCardWidth(columns)` (percentage-width formula with safety margin for the gap between cards — reproduces the app's existing, already-proven 47% value exactly at 2 columns, rather than inventing a new one).

**Phase 2 — reflowed all 6 originally-scoped grid files**: `student/creatures.tsx`, `parent/checkin.tsx` (strategy picker), `student/select.tsx` (family/student roster), `src/components/CreatureCollection.tsx` — genuine variable-length data grids, now 2/3/4 columns via `useDataGridColumns()`. `teacher/checkin.tsx` (4 emotion-zone buttons) and `student/submit-creature.tsx` (4 evolution-stage review photos) — fixed-count grids, stay 2×2 on phone, become a single row of 4 on tablet via `useFixedGridColumns()`, deliberately not a progressive reflow since 4 items across 3 columns leaves an awkward orphan.

**Real bug caught by `tsc`, not visual testing** — exactly the kind of thing live-verification is for even without a device: `gridCardWidth()` initially returned a plain `string`, which TypeScript's RN style types reject for `width` (needs the narrower percentage-literal type). Caused 8 new compile errors across every file the grid work touched. Fixed by typing the return as `` `${number}%` ``; re-ran `tsc`, back to exactly the 23-error baseline.

**Phase 3 — broader sweep (via a fork, to avoid re-grepping ~350 files into this session's own context) for anything Phase 1/2's narrower searches missed**, since Phase 2's own investigation had already shown the original search patterns (literal "47%"/"48%", style names `modalContent:`/`modalCard:`) were too narrow — found and fixed one real gap themselves (`CreatureCollection.tsx`'s modal, named `overlay`/`container` instead of the searched-for names). The broader sweep found:
- **5 more real un-capped modals**, all the same bottom-sheet shape as Phase 1's fixes, just under different style names the original grep missed: `student/zone.tsx` (`modalOverlay`/`modalContainer`), `subscription/index.tsx` (`enquiryOverlay`/`enquirySheet`), `parent/family-strategies.tsx` (inline literal styles, not a named StyleSheet), `parent/linked-child/[id].tsx` (`overlay`/`sheet`, shared by 2 separate `<Modal>` instances — one edit covers both), `src/components/CreatureDetailModal.tsx` (`overlay`/`container`). All fixed with the identical, now-proven pattern: `alignItems:'center'` on the overlay + `width:'100%', maxWidth:480` on the content.
- **One more real, non-trivial grid**: `student/world-creatures.tsx` — already used `useWindowDimensions()` (from an earlier Aug 22 redesign) but was hardcoded to a 2-column, 2-row-per-page paginated grid (`PAGE_SIZE = 4`, horizontal swipe between pages). This one needed real judgment, not just a mechanical width injection — widening the cards alone without changing page size would've put 4 items into a 3-column grid on tablet, producing an awkward 3+1 orphaned-row layout that would look worse than doing nothing. Fixed by making `PAGE_SIZE` reactive too (`columns × 2`, preserving the original "2 rows per page" design intent from the Aug 22 redesign comment, just generalized to the live column count) alongside the card-width formula.
- **Three lower-priority grids found, deliberately left alone**: `kiosk/index.tsx`, `teacher/widget.tsx`, `parent/widget.tsx` — all already reactive from Phase 1 (their pixel-based card width recomputes on resize) and already at 3-4 columns, not stuck at 2 like everything else. Adding one more column would have a real but smaller visual payoff, and conservative was the explicit instruction — left as-is rather than pushed further.
- **No genuine fixed-pixel-width container issues found** — every large numeric width in the 250-600px range was already either correctly capped or a legitimately small, fixed branding element (e.g. a 280px logo variant).

**Verified mechanically, since no device is available**: hand-checked the column-count and width-percentage math against 5 real device widths (iPhone 390pt, iPad mini 744pt, iPad/Air 820pt, iPad Pro 11" 834pt, iPad Pro 12.9" 1024pt) — confirmed no combination of columns × card-width × gaps overflows 100% on any of them, with 3.4-5.1% real margin to spare in every case; the phone case (2 columns, 47%) is numerically unchanged from the existing, already-shipped value. `npx tsc --noEmit`: 23 errors, same pre-existing baseline, confirmed after fixing the `gridCardWidth` type bug. Real `expo export --platform ios`: clean bundle.

**What genuinely needs Jono's own device/TestFlight confirmation, flagged explicitly per his instruction** (none of this can be visually verified in this environment):
- Whether 3 columns on a 744-834pt iPad and 4 columns on a 1024pt iPad Pro actually look right, not just avoid overflowing — card proportions, image/text cramping inside each card, and touch-target size haven't been seen, only measured.
- Whether the "single row of 4" treatment for the emotion-zone buttons and evolution-stage photos reads well, versus feeling like a stretched-out, overly-wide single row on the largest iPad.
- `world-creatures.tsx`'s paginated grid specifically — the page-size formula is a judgment call (kept the original "2 rows" design intent), not something derived from any visual precedent on a wider screen.
- The 5 newly-capped modals — same mechanical-correctness-but-unverified-appearance caveat as every modal from Phase 1.

## A49 — CRITICAL: systemic authorization audit (parent→teacher IDOR, subscription-tier bypass) + items 4-8, 13, 2026-08-26

Jono reported 8 real bugs from live testing, several potentially serious, with explicit instructions to investigate every one against real source before building anything beyond a quick correction. Items 1 and 2 confirmed severe enough to treat with Aug 25 auth-bypass-level urgency, fixed and verified first as their own gate before continuing to the rest.

**1. Parent access to Teacher dashboard — the Aug 26 fix was real but scoped to one screen; the actual hole was server-side and systemic.** `teacher/dashboard.tsx` itself was correctly gated (client-side redirect), but every other teacher screen (`students.tsx`, `classrooms.tsx`, `checkin.tsx`, `resources.tsx`, `strategies.tsx`, `student-detail.tsx`, `widget.tsx`) had zero role gating anywhere, client or server. Live-confirmed with a fresh test parent account against production: `GET /students` → `200 []`, `POST /students` → `200`, successfully created a real student record. A full systematic audit (same rigor as Aug 25) across every `/teacher/*` and `/family/*` endpoint found ~30 endpoints with no server-side authorization, several full IDORs (any authenticated user — not just wrong-role, any user at all — could read or write another teacher's data by guessing an id), not just role gaps:
- Full IDORs on writes: `DELETE /classrooms/{id}`, `PUT/DELETE /students/{id}`, `PUT/DELETE /custom-strategies/{id}`, `POST /custom-strategies`, `POST /students/{id}/generate-link-code`, `POST /family/members/{id}/checkin`, `POST /family/members/{id}/link-student` (could re-point ANY family_members row to any student), `PUT /strategies/sync/{id}`, `POST/DELETE/PUT /teacher/student/{id}/strategies*`, `POST /teacher/student/{id}/flag-to-admin`.
- Full IDORs on reads: `GET /students/{id}`, `GET /zone-logs?student_id=`, `GET /teacher/student/{id}/home-data` (comment literally said "verify teacher owns this student" — the code never did), `GET /teacher/student/{id}/combined-checkins`, `GET /strategies/shared/{id}`, `GET /classrooms/{id}/join-code` (anyone could read and silently generate another teacher's real classroom join code), `GET /family/zone-logs/{member_id}`, `GET /family/members/{id}/checkins`.
- Completely unauthenticated: `GET /analytics/classroom/{classroom_id}` had no `request: Request` parameter at all — impossible to even check auth. The frontend was already sending a real token; it was just never read.
- `GET /custom-strategies` with no `student_id` had no filter at all — returned every custom strategy for every student system-wide to any authenticated user.
- Role-gap-only (self-scoped already, not a leak, but let any role touch teacher/parent functionality as themselves): `GET/POST /students`, `GET/POST /classrooms`, `GET /analytics/classroom/all`, `GET/POST /family/members`.
- Found and fixed several bare `except Exception` clauses that would have silently swallowed the new 403s into misleading 500s.
- New shared `_is_authorized_for_classroom()` helper, mirroring the existing `_is_authorized_for_student()` precedent already used correctly elsewhere — extended consistently, not patched ad hoc.
- **Live-verified with 18 real tests, all passing**: parent blocked on every teacher endpoint; a real cross-teacher IDOR attempt (Teacher A reading/deleting Teacher B's real classroom and student) blocked; the legitimate owner still does everything normally (read, list, analytics, join-code, delete own records). Full cleanup confirmed.

**2. Account-switch subscription bypass — confirmed real, live-exploited.** `PUT /auth/role` only ever flipped the `role` column; `subscription_plan` is written once by the Stripe webhook and never read anywhere else in the codebase. Live-confirmed the exploit: a genuinely-paying test parent switched to `teacher` via the real endpoint, then created 11 students against the free-tier 10-student teacher cap — all 11 succeeded, cap never enforced. Fixed: switching to teacher now requires genuine entitlement via one of three legitimate paths — an actual teacher-tier Stripe subscription, a valid trial (grants full access regardless of role everywhere else in this codebase), or the account's own school already covering it as a teacher (`_teacher_is_school_covered`). Switching to parent stays ungated — it's the cheaper/free-available tier, never itself a bypass. **5 live tests, all passing**: paying parent blocked with a clear message; free parent blocked; genuine teacher-plan account succeeds; valid trial succeeds; teacher→parent always succeeds.

Both deployed and reported back to Jono as their own checkpoint before continuing, per his explicit instruction.

**3. Superadmin logout button — real, quick fix.** Cleared session state but never navigated away, so it looked like nothing happened. One-line fix matching the already-proven pattern in `settings.tsx`'s own logout (`await logout(); router.replace('/')`).

**4. Cancel-subscription false error — three unsynced sources of truth for "is this a real subscription," now reconciled.** Settings' display, `GET /subscription/status`, and `POST /subscription/cancel` each checked something different. Concrete real trigger: a school-covered teacher/parent gets `subscription_status: "active"` (correctly — they DO have access) with no `stripe_customer_id` at all, so Settings showed "Active" next to a Cancel button guaranteed to fail. Per Jono's explicit decision: Settings now hides Cancel entirely for school-covered accounts and shows "Managed by your school" instead (new `school_covered` flag on `GET /subscription/status`, computed from the same `_teacher_is_school_covered`/`_parent_is_school_covered` helpers used elsewhere). `POST /subscription/cancel` also now distinguishes the separate real case of a genuinely stale/desynced Stripe subscription with an honest message, instead of one flat error for three different situations. **Live-verified 3 scenarios on fresh test accounts**: school-covered → clear "managed by your school" message; never-subscribed → the original honest "no active subscription" message; stale `stripe_customer_id` → new "couldn't find an active payment" message. Full cleanup confirmed.

**5. Non-linked child missing time-filter pills — real, fixed.** Non-linked children route to a less-developed detail screen (`family-member-stats/[id].tsx`) that never got the period-selector feature `linked-child/[id].tsx` already has — a genuine inconsistency, not a legitimate difference, since the data (a full year of history, `?days=365`) was already being fetched and just never exposed. Added the same period pills, client-side filtered (no new backend calls) — deliberately kept the calendar/PDF-month-download section on the full year of history, not the stats period, since a calendar shouldn't shrink to "today" just because the stats pill is set to Today.

**6-8. Italian audio, stale language badges, loading freezes — investigated together since Jono suspected a shared cause; confirmed two are real and independent, one hunch didn't hold up.**
- **Audio (real bug, fixed)**: `manifestCache`/`manifestLanguage` in `voiceClips.ts` are shared module-level singletons with no language-match check. `loadVoiceManifest` fires fire-and-forget on mount and is never awaited before the screen allows interaction, so tapping a colour before a fresh fetch resolves played whatever was cached from the previous language. Backend confirmed clean beforehand (all 28 Italian clips genuinely live). `playVoiceClip` now takes the caller's current language and awaits a fresh fetch itself whenever the cache doesn't match, instead of trusting background timing.
- **Badges (real, and wrong for a second language too)**: found this was stale for `es` as well as `it`, not just the one reported — backend's `VOICE_CLIP_LANGUAGES` is `("en","pt","es","it")` after the Aug 23 Spanish and Aug 25 Italian rollouts, but the frontend array was still dated Aug 21 ("en","pt" only). `es` is a partial rollout (colour names only, no helpers yet) — marked `true` anyway, matching `pt`'s own existing partial-rollout precedent.
- **Freezing (real, but NOT connected to the audio bug — verified, not assumed)**: two independent systemic gaps. Neither the Helpers screen nor "Loading your creature" used the app's own established `EmotionColourLoader` pattern (already used in 8 other files) — added to both. The whole networking layer had zero request timeouts anywhere — added a 30s `AbortController` timeout to the shared `apiRequest()` core function (used by most of the app, including `rewards.tsx`'s chain of up to 4 sequential calls) and to `getStudentShield()` specifically, a separate raw `fetch()` sitting in the same reported loading sequence. 8 other raw `fetch()` calls in `notifications.ts` share the same gap — flagged, deliberately left untouched rather than silently expanding scope.

**13. Password/sensitive-code field redesign — researched, proposed, confirmed, built.** New shared `src/components/SecureField.tsx`, generalized from the best-built existing pattern already in the app (`settings.tsx`'s trial-code field: wrapper owns the border, input has none, in-flow eye icon) rather than inventing a new convention. Real bugs closed: 8 of 11 fields had no show/hide toggle at all; the actual "Set Password" screen with the reported double-border bug turned out to be `auth/set-password-required.tsx` (a forced first-login flow), not `settings.tsx`'s separate inline Set Password section (which was already clean) — genuinely had a border-wrapped-around-a-bordered-input double border, plus a Confirm Password field silently sharing New Password's visibility toggle state (the same shared-state bug also found and fixed in settings.tsx's own Confirm Password); the delete-account password field rendered with no visible border/box at all; `forgot-password.tsx` used a different border width/font size than every other password field. No visible focus state existed anywhere — the shared component's wrapper border now switches to the app's indigo accent on focus, with border*width* held constant so focusing never shifts layout by a pixel. PIN-entry numpads (`teacher/checkin.tsx`, `parent/my-wellbeing.tsx`) deliberately left as dot-based, no-reveal — matches the standard platform PIN convention, a different and already-appropriate pattern, confirmed with Jono before building. Migrated 11 fields across 6 files. Verified: zero raw `secureTextEntry` `TextInput`s remain in any migrated file; the component has exactly one `borderWidth` definition (the input itself has none), so a double border is now structurally impossible, not just fixed in the one reported instance.

**Live-verification summary for this whole round**: items 1, 2, and 4 verified against real production data with fresh test accounts, real positive- and negative-path assertions, and confirmed cleanup every time. Items 3, 5-8, 13 are frontend-only logic/UI fixes, verified via `npx tsc --noEmit` (23 pre-existing errors throughout, zero new) and a real `expo export --platform ios` (clean bundle) after every batch — genuine on-device visual/interactive confirmation (does a focus colour change look right, does the Helpers screen actually stop freezing on a real network) isn't possible in this environment and is explicitly still Jono's to confirm.

Committed and pushed in stages: `9c4d070f` (item 2), `4634535a` (item 1, the full authorization audit), `3100d3ac` (items 3-8), `d442bfae` (item 13).

Not touched, deliberately out of scope: the 3 lower-priority grids noted above; anything not surfaced by either sweep.

## A50 — Items 9-12, cross-tenant admin_settings fix (CRITICAL), Resend password-reset email, PIN inventory, 2026-08-27

**Item 9 — App Schools tab parity.** Investigated the portal's real `schoolManagementHTML`/`loadSASchools` implementation first (per Jono's instruction, same standard as item 7). Portal already had full expand/edit/collapse; the app's `SchoolsManager` had expand/collapse but no Edit at all, and — a deeper structural gap — sourced its list from `stats.schools_breakdown` (check-in activity) instead of `/admin/school-profiles` (the real registered-schools CRUD source), meaning a school with zero check-ins yet was invisible and uneditable, same separation-of-concerns problem the portal itself fixed on Aug 26. Fixed: list now sources from `/admin/school-profiles` like the portal, real Edit added with the full field set (was missing subscription_package/seats/renewal/status/notes), status badge added, check-in counts still shown as supplementary info via the same merge-by-name the old code used.

**Item 10 — Superadmin user-card stat badges + filters, app and portal.** Design fork surfaced before building broadly (per Jono's explicit ask): subscription-status badge, days-to-renewal, copy-email, and usage-frequency needed real decisions, not defaults. Confirmed live: `/admin/users` already returns `subscription_status`/`subscription_expires_at`/`school_admin_id` — badge and days-left computed client-side, no backend change. Usage frequency has no existing per-user aggregate and means something different per role (teacher: own `teacher_checkins`; parent: no self check-ins, only their linked child's via `parent_links → feeling_logs`) — Jono's calls: show the linked child's frequency for parents (not omit), build a real bulk aggregation endpoint (not N+1), and for the school-covered badge use the LOOSE `school_admin_id`-is-set definition (matching `check_subscription_active`, what the user actually experiences) as primary, with a secondary "⚠️ lapsed" flag when that school's own subscription isn't active. New `GET /admin/users/checkin-frequency` — one bulk call, buckets 7/14/30-day counts server-side from `teacher_checkins`/`feeling_logs`, returns a flat `{user_id: {...}}` map. Portal's role/school/country filters already existed (built during item 8 polish); added missing school/country filters to the app's `UsersManager` via a `FilterPickerButton` modal component (no native `<select>` in RN). Both surfaces built in parallel for sync.

**Item 11 — CRITICAL security fix, promo-code CRUD, scoped/redacted export, suspend/reactivate.**
- **`admin_settings` cross-tenant leak — genuinely live, confirmed exploitable before the fix, fixed first as its own gate per Jono's explicit priority.** The table is shared by 3 different conventions (`school_admin_id`+`setting_key`/`setting_value` for notification prefs — correctly scoped; `school_admin_id`+`key`/`value` for `/schools/my-school` — correctly scoped; bare `key`/`value` for `/admin/settings`, `/school/register`, `/school/profile` — **not scoped at all**). Any school_admin could read/overwrite ANY global key via the generic `/admin/settings`, and `/school/register`/`/school/profile` (dead — zero callers anywhere in frontend/portal/marketing site, confirmed by grep and live curl, but still reachable directly) let every school_admin's registration clobber the SAME global rows every other school_admin's read from. **Live-confirmed the exploit before fixing**: the demo school_admin's (Sunshine International School) `/school/profile` call returned **Kairos Montessori's** data. Fixed: `/admin/settings` locked to superadmin-only (matches its real use — `wellbeing_email`, a genuinely global key); `/school/register`/`/school/profile` scoped to `school_admin_id`; `account_suspended` (see below) enforced in real time inside `get_current_user()` itself, not just at login. **Live re-verified post-deploy, same account**: `/school/profile` → correctly Sunshine now; `/admin/export?type=checkins` → 840 (was 1000, the whole platform) — 840 matches Sunshine's own real historical total from A49; `/admin/export?type=users` → 4, all Sunshine-only, `portal_password` confirmed stripped; `/admin/promo-codes`/`/admin/settings` → correctly 403 for school_admin.
- **Promo-code CRUD** (`/admin/promo-codes` GET/POST/PUT/DELETE, superadmin-only) — replaces the hardcoded `PROMO_CODES` dict (required a deploy to change). New `promo_codes` table (migration below), `/subscription/redeem-trial-code` checks it first, falls back to the hardcoded dict so the 2 original codes keep working regardless of migration state. **Live-verified full round-trip post-migration** (real superadmin session): create → real DB row; list → alongside the 2 built-ins; **redeem** as a different real account → real 7-day trial applied, `uses` incremented 0→1; deactivate → `is_active` false; redeem again → correctly rejected `400 "no longer active"`; delete → gone, list back to exactly the 2 built-ins, no orphaned state.
- **`/admin/export`** — was a fully unscoped, unredacted, JSON-only full-table dump (confirmed live pre-fix: school_admin got 1000 platform-wide checkins). Now: school_admin scoped to their own school (same `teacher_ids`/`classroom_owner_ids` pattern as `/school-admin/analytics`), superadmin unrestricted; `date_from`/`date_to` filtering; real `format=csv` (was previously just a JSON response labelled "csv"); `users` export redacted through the existing `_public_user()` sanitizer (`portal_password`/`reset_token` stripped).
- **Account suspend/reactivate** (`POST /admin/users/{id}/suspend` / `/reactivate`, superadmin-only) — new `account_suspended`/`suspended_at`/`suspended_by`/`suspension_reason` columns (migration below). Enforced in real time via `get_current_user()` (kills an already-active session immediately, not just blocks the next login) plus a clear message at `/auth/email-login`, mirroring the existing `deletion_requested_at` precedent. Self-suspend and suspending-a-superadmin both blocked. Wired into the Users list (app + portal) rather than a separate email-lookup tool in Settings, since `user_id` is already in memory there. **Live-verified full round-trip** using a disposable `/admin/create-school-admin`-created test account (zero risk to real demo accounts, per Jono's explicit request after flagging the original test plan would have used a live demo account): baseline login 200 → suspend → login 403 "account has been suspended" → reactivate → login 200, all suspension fields correctly cleared. Self-suspend guard also verified: `400 "You can't suspend your own account"`.

Migration (run by Jono, confirmed complete):
```sql
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN account_suspended boolean DEFAULT false;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN suspended_at timestamptz;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN suspended_by text;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN suspension_reason text;
CREATE TABLE promo_codes (
  code text PRIMARY KEY, type text DEFAULT 'trial', days integer DEFAULT 30,
  max_uses integer, uses integer DEFAULT 0, is_active boolean DEFAULT true,
  expires_at timestamptz, notes text, created_by text, created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()
);
```

**Item 12 — Creature press-and-hold progress ring.** Design fork surfaced before building (per Jono's ask): % complete means something different for the two creature types that can be "active" on `world-creatures.tsx` — default creatures are points-based (fixed 0/25/60/120 thresholds, identical across all 4 lines), community (student-submitted, approved) creatures are a rolling 30-day check-in count against `[5,10,15,20]`. Jono's call: add `progress_percent` to `GET /students/{id}/active-creatures` (single source of truth computed server-side with the exact same thresholds that decide real evolution, rather than duplicating the math client-side), radial ring over a bar. Built `_creature_progress_percent()` reusing existing data (`student_rewards.creature_points` / `feeling_logs` count, both already-fetched shapes), `ProgressRing` (react-native-svg `Circle` + `strokeDashoffset`, already a dependency, previously unused anywhere in the app), `Pressable` `onPressIn`/`onPressOut` driving an `Animated` opacity fade (120ms in, 250ms out) on the active card's image overlay. Added a small "👆 hold the picture to see progress" hint under the existing active-creature text so the affordance is discoverable.

**Real password-reset email delivery (Resend), built after the security fix surfaced the gap.** The Aug 26 fix (A49-adjacent, see reset_password_request's own history) correctly stopped leaking the reset token in the API response, but left self-service reset genuinely non-functional — token generated, never delivered anywhere. Chose Resend over SendGrid for this volume (single pip install, ~5-line send call, sandbox mode sends immediately with zero DNS setup — good enough to build and verify same-night, upgradeable to arbitrary real users later just by verifying a sending domain, no code change needed). `RESEND_API_KEY` set on Railway (via CLI, value never displayed/retained per Jono's explicit instruction on custody of secrets this session — same standard applied to the ADMIN_PIN retrieval below). Defensive fallback intact: missing key or a failed send falls back to the exact Aug 26 behavior (log a warning, generic response) rather than erroring. **Live-verified real end-to-end delivery**: `POST /auth/reset-password-request` for jono@classofhappiness.com → Resend send call succeeded (id captured from Railway logs, never from any API response) → polled Resend's own `GET /emails/{id}` status API → `last_event: "delivered"`, not just "sent"/"accepted". Security invariant re-confirmed unchanged: response identical regardless of account existence, never includes the token in any API response or log line — it only reaches the owner via the email itself now. Jono to complete the human-in-the-loop half himself (open the real email, use the code, confirm new password works and old one doesn't) since reading an actual inbox isn't something this environment can do.

**PIN system inventory — investigated per Jono's explicit request, all 6 distinct systems.** School admin / teacher / parent login PINs (items 1-3) **don't exist as real per-account features** — only the 4 hardcoded `ALWAYS_OPEN_PINS` demo/test emails get a PIN prompt at all (confirmed via `PIN_REQUIRED_EMAILS` in `login.tsx` and the backend's own docstring: *"Real (non-demo) school_admin accounts have no PIN infrastructure yet"*). Superadmin `ADMIN_PIN` (item 4): reconfirmed — bare Railway env var, two check sites (`/auth/email-login`, `/admin/verify`), zero in-app recovery. Teacher check-in PIN (item 5) for "My Wellbeing": **doesn't exist** — that screen is parent-only, no teacher equivalent anywhere in the codebase. Parent wellbeing PIN (item 6): genuinely separate mechanism, `AsyncStorage`-only (`wellbeing_pin_${memberId}`), never touches the server — and already has a real, working Reset PIN button (not just a hint), so not a gap. **Decision (Jono): leave ADMIN_PIN as-is** — exactly one superadmin account exists, Railway CLI access already works as real recovery (proven live this session), not worth building dedicated infrastructure for a user base of one. **Logged as a future task**: if a second superadmin account is ever created, build the real fix — move ADMIN_PIN off the env var onto a DB column on the superadmin's own `users` row (`admin_pin_hash`, same shape as `portal_password`), rewire the two check sites, add set/reset endpoints, which then reuses the Resend flow already built tonight almost for free. Same note applies if real per-role (school admin/teacher/parent) login PINs are ever built as a genuine feature — they'd naturally be a new `users` column shaped like `portal_password` and should reuse the same Resend email-reset pattern rather than inventing a separate design.

**On retrieving secrets this session**: Jono explicitly authorized using Railway CLI access (already linked/authenticated) to retrieve `ADMIN_PIN` directly for live superadmin verification, with the explicit instruction not to display or retain the value in any report back to him — followed throughout (values fetched into shell variables and used immediately, never echoed, temp files containing derived session tokens deleted after each verification pass). `RESEND_API_KEY` was pasted directly by Jono and set on Railway the same way, same non-display standard applied.

## A53 — Copy-email crash fix, item 6 (multi-school PDF), demo-prep items 2-4 (seed data, analytics, card stack), two real bugs found live-verifying, 2026-08-28

**Copy-email crash on the Users list — real device bug, fixed.** Bare `import('expo-clipboard')` had no synchronous guard - if the native module isn't linked in the running build, resolving it can throw before the promise chain's own `.catch()` attaches. Matched the already-proven-safe pattern from `settings.tsx`'s own copy-code button (legacy RN `Clipboard` first, optional-chained, falls back to `expo-clipboard`), wrapped in a real try/catch. Now: `school_names` (comma-separated) picks specific schools; no selection defaults to every REAL registered school (`school_profiles`), not a flattened aggregate - 2+ schools render a real comparison table plus a full detail section per school.
